16
tamiḻ ārāycci
muṉṉurai
tamiḻaippaṟṟiya ārāycci nūlkaḷ mikaccilavē veḷivan tuḷḷaṉa. avaikaḷuṭ perumpālāṉa vaṭamoḻiyaiyē tamatu tāy moḻiyeṉa apimāṉikkiṉṟavarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭavai. ākavē avaikaḷuḷ nām tamiḻaippaṟṟiya uṇmai varalāṟukaḷaik kāṇutal aritākiṉṟatu. ikkaruttiṉaiyē intiya makkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟai ārāynta ayalnāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ mēlum mēlum veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. tamiḻ ārāycci eṉpatu; palkalaik kaḻakattilō, vēṟu ārāyccik kaḻakattilō, patavi ēṟṟu vēlaipuriyum paṭṭatārikaḷ cilarāl māttiram ceyyattakkatu; ēṉaiyar ataṉaic ceyyum āṟṟal cālātavarkaḷ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa karuttukkaḷum nilavukiṉṟaṉa. iṉit tamiḻ ārāycciyil aṟiñar palar, nuḻaiyāmaikkup piṟa kāraṇamu muṇṭu. ittuṟaiyil varunti uḻaippavarkaḷukkup pōtiya ūtiyam kiṭaippatillai. tamiḻ vaḷarccikkākavē irukkiṉṟaṉaveṉac collik koḷḷum nilaiyaṅkaḷum avarkaḷukku utaviyaḷittu ūkkam kāṭṭuvatillai. tamiḻ aṟivucāṉṟa tiru. kā. cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷai, M.A.M.L. avar kaḷukkē cila puttaka veḷiyīṭṭāḷarkaḷ oru pakkattukku oru rūpāy vītam ūtiyam aḷittu nūlkaḷiṉ patippurimaiyaip peṟṟārkaḷāṉāl, cātāraṇa ārāycciyāḷariṉ nilaimai eṉṉa eṉpataic caṟṟē cintittup pāruṅkaḷ. iṉṟu tamiḻ ārāycci eṉpatu ōraḷavil araciya lōṭu toṭarpu paṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻar tammaiyum tamatu moḻiyaiyum paṟṟiya ellā uṇmai varalāṟukaḷaiyum paṟṟi aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭi yirukkiṉṟatu. ākavē potu makkaḷ uṇmaiyai aṟiyak kūṭiya muṟai yil innūl veḷivarukiṉṟatu.
ceṉṉai
21.3.1947 na.ci. kantaiyā
tamiḻ ārāycci
tōṟṟuvāy
tamiḻaippaṟṟiyum, tamiḻaraippaṟṟiyum tavaṟāṉa karuttukkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ paraviyuḷḷaṉa. itaṟkuk kāraṇam, tamiḻ camakkirutattiṉ vaḻimoḻi eṉṟum, tamiḻariṉ nākarikam, vaṭa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum teṉ ṉāṭṭiṟku vanta pirāmaṇa vakuppiṉarāṟ kaṭṭi eḻuppappaṭṭatu eṉṟum palar eḻuti vantamaiyēyākum. ārāycciyil ik karuttukkaḷ muṟṟum tavaṟuṭaiyaṉa. iṉṟu ik karuttukkaḷai eṭuttu āḷuvōr, uṇmai aṟiyamāṭṭātu paḻam pōkkiṉar eṉa otukkappaṭuvar. tamiḻaraiyum tamiḻaiyum paṟṟiya varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟukaḷ pala kiṭaittuḷḷa ikkālat til āriyattāl tamiḻ vaḷam aṭaintatu; āriyap pirāmaṇarāl tamiḻar nākarikam aṭaintārkaḷ eṉa nāṭṭa oruvar muṉ varuvārēl, avar pūmi uruṇṭai vaṭiviṉataṉṟu; taṭṭai vaṭiviṉatē eṉa nāṭṭuvataṟku muṉ vantavarāvar. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ārāycci, tiruvaḷḷuvar ārāycci eṉat tamiḻar perumaikaḷai nāṭṭuvaṉapōṉṟa kaṭṭuraikaḷaiyō ciṟu nūlkaḷaiyō eḻutum ciṟ cilar, tamiḻiṉ ākkattukkum tamiḻariṉ uyarvukkum vaṭamoḻiyum vaṭamoḻiyāḷarumē kāraṇam eṉak kuṟippākavō, veḷippaṭaiyākavō tōṉṟumpaṭi eḻuti varukiṉṟaṉar. an nūlkaḷaip payilum nammavar palar uṇmaiyaṟiyātu peritum taṭumāṟukiṉṟaṉar. uṇmaiyil tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum ākkam aḷittavarkaḷ āriyarēyāyiṉ, avvāṟu kūṟuvatil iḻukku yātum illai. ārāycciyil āriyarukku nākarikattaiyum, avar moḻikku eḻuttaiyum, avarkaḷ payilvataṟkup pala nūlkaḷaiyum ceytu aḷittavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷāyiruppatai varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ teḷivāka ārāyntu kūṟiyirukka, avaikaḷaip payiṉṟu uṇmai teḷiyātu eḻuti varum ciṟa cilar karuttukkaḷiṉ ovvāmaiyait teḷivuṟutta vēṇṭi, icciṟu nūl veḷivaruvatu avaciyamākiṉṟatu.
tirāviṭam
intiya nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷuḷ mika mikap paḻamai yuṭaiyatu tamiḻ. vaṭakkē imayamalaiyum, teṟkē kaṉṉiyākumariyum, kiḻakkum mēṟkum kaṭalumākiya ellaikaḷuk kuṭpaṭṭa nāṭukaḷuḷ oru kāl tamiḻmoḻi oṉṟē vaḻaṅkiṟṟu1. āriyar varukaikkuppiṉ vaṭakkē vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻ, pala vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷaiyum ataṉ citaivukaḷaiyum ēṟṟup pala moḻikaḷāka māṟutalaṭaintatu. ammoḻikaḷ āriyattukku iṉamuṭaiyaṉa eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl uṇmai ataṟku nērmāṟāṉatu.
1teṟkē vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻ uṟaḻntum piṟaḻntum kaṉṉaṭam, teluṅku, tuḷu, malaiyāḷam eṉṉum moḻikaḷāka māṟiyuḷḷaṉa. immoḻi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil āriyaccoṟkaḷai ammoḻikaḷil cērttu vaḻaṅkuvataṟkut taṉippaṭṭa vitikaḷ uṇṭu. itaṉāl ammoḻikaḷiṉ, toṭakkattil āriyac coṟkaḷ irukkavillai yeṉṉum. piṟkālattil avai cērkkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟum naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, tuḷu, malaiyāḷam, tamiḻ eṉṉum moḻikaḷ aṭaṅkiya kūṭṭattukku moḻi nūlār tirāviṭam eṉap peyariṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.
tirāviṭam eṉṉum peyar, muṉ pūmi cāttiram campantamākat teṉṉāṭṭuk kaṭaṟkarai nāṭukaḷaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉṟum, tirai iṭam eṉappaṭṭatē tirāviṭam eṉat tirintateṉṟum cermaṉ āciriyar oruvar ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
vaṭaintiya moḻikaḷ, amaippil tirāviṭamāyiruppataṟkuk kāraṇam
intiya nāṭṭai aṭainta āriyariṉ tokai aṅku vāḻntu koṇṭirunta makkaḷaiviṭac ciṟu tokaiyākum. 2ākavē avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya moḻi aṅku vāḻntu koṇṭirunta makkaḷiṉ moḻi amaippil māṟṟam uṇṭākka muṭiyavillai. āṉāl āriyac coṟkaḷ pala kalantu ammoḻiyait tiripaṭaiyacceytaṉa. pala tirāviṭaccoṟ kaḷum āriya moḻiyil ceṉṟu ēṟiṉa. ṭākṭar kiṟṟal eṉpār (Dr. Kittel) tamatu kaṉṉaṭa akarātiyiṉ muṉṉuraiyil vaṭamoḻi, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiliruntu iraval peṟṟateṉat karutappaṭum 420 coṟkaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. vaṭanāṭṭil vaḻaṅkuvaṉavum āriyattuk kiṉamuṭaiyavai eṉṟu karutappaṭuvaṉavumākiya moḻikaḷ, amaippil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷai ottaṉa eṉa, pi.ti. srīṉivāca aiyaṅkār avarkaḷum tamatu intiyāviṉ kaṟkālam, tamiḻar varalāṟu eṉṉum nūlkaḷil viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇam orōriṭattut tamiḻ ilakkaṇattai ottiruttaliṉ kāraṇam
āriyar intiya nāṭṭai aṭaintapōtu avarkaḷiṉ moḻi mikat tiruntamaṟṟatākaviruntatu. mikap paḻaiya pāṭalkaḷil kāṇappaṭum āriyac coṟkaḷ camakkirutattiṉum pārkkak kirēkka moḻippōkkākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu māk° mūlar kūṟiyirukkiṟār. āriyar anākarikarāka varumpōtu nakaraṅkaḷil vāḻntu koṇṭirunta tirāviṭa makkaḷ, avarkaḷukku eḻuttukkaḷai utaviyum, avar moḻiyait tiruttiyamaittum tuṇaipurintārkaḷ. ākavē vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ pala tamiḻ uccarippai aṭaintatōṭu ataṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ cilavum tamiḻ ilakkaṇattai ottaṉavāyiṉa. 1ivvuṇmaiyai aṟiya māṭṭāta ārāycciyāḷar, tamiḻar āriyariṭattiliruntu cila ilakkaṇa vitikaḷai iraval peṟṟārkaḷ eṉak kūṟuvārāyiṉar. (oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ māṟalām; ataṉ ilakkaṇattait taṉippaṭṭa cilar māṟṟa muṭiyāteṉa moḻi ārāycci vallār2 kūṟuvar).
tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭṭāl avai vaṭamoḻikkuriyaṉavā?
oru kūṭṭattiṉar vaṭamoḻi kaṭavuḷ moḻi eṉṟum, atu piṟamoḻiyiliruntu coṟkaḷai iravalpeṟa māṭṭāteṉṟum nampikkaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē ak kūṭṭattiṉar tamiḻiṟ kāṇappaṭum ceṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉavāyiṉ avai vaṭacoṟkaḷ eṉṟē kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. vaṭamoḻi pala, muṇṭā moḻic coṟkaḷaiyum, tirāviṭamoḻic ceṟkaḷaiyum iraval peṟṟirukkiṉṟateṉpatai ārāycciyaḷar kuṟippiṭṭuḷ ḷārkaḷ. piṟi°lu°ki eṉpar muṇṭā moḻic coṟkaḷ pala vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pukuntiruppataik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. kaṉṉaṭa akarātiyait tokutta “ṭākṭar” kiṟṟal (Dr.Kittel) vaṭamoḻi tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiliruntu iraval peṟṟateṉak karutappaṭum 420 coṟkaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭi yuḷḷār. kālṭvel pātiriyār vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ceṉṟuḷḷa pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷaik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. tamiḻilum, āriyattilum kāṇappaṭum coṟkaḷ intu-cermaṉ moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavō eṉṟu pārttal vēṇṭum. avai ammoḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭuvaṉavāyiṉ avai āriya moḻikkuriyaṉavē. intu-cermaṉ moḻikaḷukkum intu āriya moḻikkum potu vallāta piṟa coṟkaḷ intiya moḻikaḷi liruntu āriyattiṟ ceṉṟēṟiyuḷḷaṉavākum.iṉṟu nām āriyac coṟkaḷ eṉak karutuvaṉa pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷ eṉa aṟiyavarukiṉṟaṉa.1 āriyar mattiya veppanilaiyuṭaiya nāṭukaḷiliruntu intiya nāṭṭai aṭaintārkaḷ. avarkaḷuṭaiya nāṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭāta marañceṭi kaḷum, vilaṅku paṟavaikaḷum piṟa poruḷkaḷum intiya nāṭṭiluḷḷaṉa. ākavē avarkaḷ apporuḷkaḷaik kuṟikka intiya makkaḷ payaṉ paṭuttiya peyarkaḷaiyē āṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
intiyar nākarikam eṉpatu tirāviṭar nākarikamē
iṉṟu intiya nākarikameṉṟu ciṟappākak kūṟappaṭuvataṟkuriya pakutikaḷ tirāviṭaruṭaiyaṉavē; ivaṟṟai varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ naṉku ārāyntu nāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ2
1“kiṟittu piṟappataṟku muṉṉum āriyar varukaikku muṉṉum tirāviṭa nākarikam, poruḷātāra, ilakkiya, tirumaṇa muṟaikaḷil naṉku vaḷarcciyuṟṟiruntaṉaveṉak koḷvatil yātum mayakkam illai.”
ṭākṭar. ē.kē. āṉantak kumāracāmi
2āriya nākarikam peritum tirāviṭa nākarikac cārpu peṟṟa teṉpatil ciṟitum cantēkam illai!! - pērāciriyar rāpcaṉ
3intiya nākarikattil āriyarallāta makkaḷuṭaiya nākarikattiṉ aṭippaṭai peritum uḷḷateṉpataic cilvaṉ levi, piṟi°lu°ki (Sylvann Levi and Jean Prsyluski) eṉpōr teḷḷitil viḷakki eḻutiyuḷ ḷārkaḷ. avarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷataṉ āṅkila moḻi peyarppaik kākci (P.Cagci) eṉpavar tāmeḻutiya āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa makkaḷ eṉṉum nūlil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. intiya varalāṟṟil, āriyarallāta makkaḷiṉ nākarika aṭippaṭai uḷḷateṉpatai ellā āciriyarkaḷum ēṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. ṭākṭar. e°.kē. caṭṭēci
4ār.ṭi.paṉērjiyum kāṭṭiyavāṟu arappā mokañcatarō nākarikam āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭatum, āriyarallāta makkaḷuṭaiyatum ākum. palucci°tāṉattil pirākūy makkaḷ kāṇappaṭutalaik koṇṭu annākarikattaik kaṭṭi eḻuppiyavarkaḷ paḻaiya tirāviṭarkaḷāvar eṉat tuṇiyappaṭukiṟatu- mēṟpaṭi
5“tirāviṭa nākarikattai nām oru pōtum kuṟaivākak karututal kūṭātu. tirāviṭa nākarikattiṉ aṭippaṭaiyiṉāl āriya nākarikam ceḻippum āḻamum eytiṟṟu. tirāviṭar pala kalaikaḷil tiṟamai aṭaintiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ pāṭavum kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ amaikkavum vallavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar” - tākūr.
camakkirutam tamiḻiṉ vēṟupāṭu
camakkirutam eṉpataṟku naṉṟākac ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉpatu poruḷ. camakkirutattukku vaṭa tamiḻ eṉpatu iṉṉoru peyar. vaṭakkē atika pirākirutac coṟkaḷai ēṟṟu vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻē naṉṟākac ceyyappaṭṭuc camakkirutam eṉṉum peyar peṟṟateṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu. caṭṭampi cāmi eṉṉum ciṟanta ārāycci aṟiñar, camakkirutat tukku aṭippaṭai tamiḻē eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār6.
“kālañceṉṟa caṭṭampi cāmikaḷiṉ kaiyeḻuttup pirati oṉṟai yāṉ pārttuḷḷēṉ. caṭṭampi cāmi avarkaḷ, kalviyāḷarkaḷ viyakkakkūṭiya paranta aṟivum ārāycciyum uṭaiyarāy viḷaṅkiṉār. camakkirutam eṉpataṟku naṉṟākac ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉpatu poruḷ. etiliruntu atu naṉṟākac ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉa avar kēṭkiṉṟār. avar pala vakaikaḷāl camakkirutattukku aṭippaṭai tamiḻ eṉa ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. teḷivākak kūṟumiṭattu ataṉ aṭippaṭai paḻantamiḻ” -irāma varmā ārāyccik kaka veḷiyīṭu
tamiḻ ārāycci, vaṭamoḻi ārāycciyāka māṟiyamai
mēlnāṭṭu makkaḷ vantu moḻikaḷaippaṟṟi ārāyntu uṇmai kāṇuvataṉ muṉpu camakkirutattiliruntē ellā moḻikaḷum uṇṭāyiṉa; ammoḻiyil uḷḷa nūlkaḷil ellāk kalaikaḷum uḷḷaṉa eṉṉum mūṭakkoḷkai makkaḷiṭaiyē paraviyiruntatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil oru kūṭṭattiṉar camakkirutam tamatu tāy moḻiyeṉak karuti vantārkaḷ; varukiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ, ārāycci celkiṉṟa vaḻiyil cella māṭṭārkaḷ; ārāycci evvāṟintālum camakkirutattiliruntu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa; āriya makkaḷ tirāviṭaraic cīrtiruttiṉar eṉṉum paḻam piṭiyaic ciṟitum kaiviṭamāṭṭārkaḷ. ivvakai maṉappāṉmai yuṭaiyavarkaḷē perumpālum tamiḻaraiyum tamiḻaiyum paṟṟi ārāyntu nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. ik kāraṇaṅkaḷiṉāl tamiḻ ārāycci ceyvōr uṇmai kāṇamāṭṭātavarkaḷākiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻaiyē tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭuḷḷa cilarum, tamiḻ, vaṭamoḻiyiṉāl ēṟṟamaṭaintatu eṉa eḻuti varukiṉṟaṉar. aṉṉavar aṟiyamaikku yām iraṅka vēṇṭiyataṉṟi avarkaḷaik kuṟai kūṟuvatāṟ payaṉillai.
tamiḻ ārāycci ceyvatu eppaṭi?
tamiḻ ārāycci ceyvataṉ muṉ tamiḻ, āriyam eṉṉum moḻi kaḷuḷ emmoḻi muṟpaṭṭatu? āriyar intiya nāṭṭai aṭaivataṉ muṉ, āriyar tamiḻar eṉṉum iru makkaḷiṉ nākarika nilai evvakai yiṉatu? anākarika maṭaintirunta makkaḷiṭamiruntu nākarika makkaḷ cilavaṟṟai iraval peṟuvārkaḷā? peruntokaiyāṉa makkaḷiṭaiyē ciṟu kūṭṭattiṉar kalakka nērntāl emmakkaḷiṉ koḷkaikaḷ karut tukkaḷ mēlōṅkum eṉpavai pōṉṟavaikaḷai ārāyntu teḷital vēṇṭum. appoḻutē ārāyccikkaṇ tiṟakkum.
mayil, kaḻakam eṉpavai vaṭacoṟkaḷā?
aṇmaiyil ārāycciyāḷar oruvar 1mayil, kaḻakam mutaliya coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, avai vaṭamoḻikkē uriyaṉa eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār. avvaṟiñar tamiḻ ārāyccit tuṟaiyil nīṇṭakālam iruntavarāyiṉum, vaṭamoḻiyil pala tirāviṭar coṟkaḷ ceṉṟu ēṟiyuḷḷaṉa eṉpataic ciṟitum aṟiyātu pōṉamai mikavum varuntattakka toṉṟākum. itu āḻntu ārāyavēṇṭiya toṉṟu aṉṟāyiṉum, itu kāraṇamākat tamiḻ māṇavar uḷḷattil tōṉṟum mayakkattaip pōkkavēṇṭiyē itaṉai īṇṭu ārāya vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu.
āriyamakkaḷ mattiya āciyāviṉiṉṟum vantavarkaḷ āvar. avar nāṭukaḷil mayil illai. mayil intiya nāṭṭukku uriya paṟavai. āriya makkaḷ tām muṉṉaṟiyātirunta marañceṭikaḷ vilaṅkukaḷ paṟavaikaḷai yum piṟavaṟṟaiyum uṇartta intiya moḻic coṟkaḷaiyē peritum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉap piṟi°lu°ki pōṉṟa ārāycci aṟiñar naṉku ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. intiyanāṭṭup paṟavaikku intiyamoḻi kaḷil peyar iruntatu. vaṭamoḻiyiṟceṉṟu vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷ eṉa ṭākṭar kiṟṟel eṉpavarāl kāṭṭappaṭṭa pala coṟkaḷuḷ mayūram oṉṟākum.* mayil eṉṉum collē vaṭamoḻiyil mayūramākat tirintu vaḻaṅki yirukkalām. vētapāṭalkaḷil mayil irukkumāṉāl atu eppaṭi vaṭamoḻikkuriyatākum. vēta moḻiyē uccarippu muṟaiyil tirāviṭac cāyal peṟṟiruntateṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vētakāla muṉivarkaḷ palar tirāviṭar eṉṟu “mu°lim kaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa intiyā” eṉṉum nūlāciriyarākiya iraṅkāccāriyar pōṉṟa aṟiñar kūṟiyuḷ ḷārkaḷ. mayilē illāta oru nāṭṭiliruntu vanta makkaḷ, mayilaik kuṟikka oru peyaraik kaṇṭupiṭittārkaḷ eṉṟum, appeyarait tamiḻ makkaḷ avarkaḷiṭamiruntu iraval peṟṟu ap paṟavaikkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟutal ārāyccik kuṟaiyēyākum. icciṟiya niyāyattaip pērāciriyar oruvar aṟintu koḷḷātatu varuntattakkatē yākum. mayil eṉṉum col maṟṟaiya āriya moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatā? intiya... āriya moḻiyil māttiram atu kāṇappaṭumāyiṉ, ammoḻikku atu evvāṟu kiṭaittatu eṉpatupōṉṟa ārāycci ceyvataṉṟō ārāyum muṟaiyākum.
kaḻakam eṉṉum collum vaṭamoḻikkuriyateṉa mēṟpaṭi āciriyar kūṟuvārāyiṉar. tamiḻil kaḷam eṉṉum col irukkumpōtu, kaḻakam eṉṉum col kaḷam eṉpatiliruntu vantateṉṟu ēṉ kūṟutal kūṭātu. coṟkaḷ uccarippu muṟaiyil ciṟitu vēṟupaṭṭu vevvēṟu karuttukkaḷai viḷakkuvatu ellā moḻikaḷukkum potuvāṉa iyalpaṉṟō. “vē” vētamāka māṭṭātu “vit” vētamākum eṉa oru cārār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉai ottatē kaḻakam vaṭamoḻi eṉak kūṟutalumākum, paḻaiya āriya moḻikaḷil kaḻakam eṉṉum col uṇṭā? kāliṉ° eṉṉum aṟiñar, āriyac coṟkaḷ eṉṟu potumakkaḷum paṇṭitarum karutum coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟai ārāyntu avai tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ eṉak kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
mokañcatarō makkaḷ tirāviṭarallarā?
avvārāycciyāḷar mokañcatarō makkaḷ tirāviṭar eṉpa taṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ illaiyeṉṟum, hera°pātiriyār, mokañcarō muttiraikaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷai vācittu, avvācippiṉ mūlam aṅku vāḻntavarkaḷ tirāviṭar eṉakkūṟutal koḷḷattakkataṉṟu eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟār. eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vācippiṉāl māttiramaṉṟu; piṟa ārāyccikaḷālum mokañcatarō makkaḷ tirāviṭarēyeṉa uṟutippaṭu kiṉṟatu. ji.ār.haṇṭar, ār.ṭi.paṉērji, pantārkkar, rātā kumutā mukkarji, pōṉṟa ciṟanta ārāycci aṟiñar vaṭanāṭu, muḻumaiyum tirāviṭamoḻi vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉṟum, cintuveḷi makkaḷiṉ nākarikam, tirāviṭa nākarikam eṉṟum naṉku ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. aṉṉōr avvāṟu tuṇivataṟkuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷa kāraṇaṅkaḷai ārāyntu maṟukkātum, tamatu kūṟṟukkuc cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭātum vāḷā an nākarikam evaruṭaiyateṉa viḷakkamākavillai eṉa oruvar kūṟuvārā ṉāl, akkūṟṟu avariṉ ārāyccik kuṟai pāṭṭait teḷḷitiṟ pulap paṭuttuvatākum. mokañcatarō arappā muttiraikaḷil kāṇappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷai ottavai varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa teṉṉintiya camātikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil poṟikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avvakai eḻuttukkaḷ ilaṅkaiyil kēkālai eṉṉum iṭattiluḷḷa malaiyilum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mecapatēmiyāviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa muttiraikaḷilum avvakai eḻuttukkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mokañca tarōvai aṭutta palucci°tāṉattil tirāviṭamoḻit toṭarpuṭaiya pirākūymoḻi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. cumēriyarum, tirāviṭarum orē iṉa makkaḷ eṉa ṭākṭar hāl kūṟiyuḷḷār. pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷ mokañ catarō eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu tōṉṟiyavai eṉṟu pērāciriyar lāṅṭaṉ ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷār. 1hera° pātiriyār oli muṟaiyāka vācitta mokañcatarō eḻuttukkaḷiṉ uccarippukkaḷ tamiḻc coṟkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai pōṉṟa pala kāraṇaṅkaḷāl mokañcatarō nākarikam tamiḻaruṭaiyatu eṉṟu naṉku teḷivākiṉṟatu. ivar mokañ catarō nākarikam toṭarpāka yātum aṟiyātiruppatu ivar kuṟaiyē yākum.
irātā kumuta mukkarji eṉṉum pērāciriyar kūṟuvatu varumāṟu:- cintu veḷi nākarikam ivvāṟu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaip pēciya makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu peṟṟiruntateṉat tōṉṟum. tirāviṭamakkaḷ mattiyataraic cāti eṉṉum kuḻuvaic cērntavarkaḷāvār. in nākarikam vētakālattukku muṟpaṭṭa intiyanākarikamākum. piṟkāla intiya nākarikattil itaṉ cila pakutikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 1767ām āṇṭu varaiyil em.ī.tāma° (ā.nu. kūhṭiayaḷa) eṉpār, āriyar contamākat tamatu eḻuttukkaḷai uṇṭākkik koḷḷavillai eṉṟum, āṉāl avarkaḷ ataṉai intiya makkaḷiṭamiruntu aṟintu koṇṭārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟuvārāyiṉar. ikkaruttu nīṇṭakālam ārāycciyāḷarāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭavillai. pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷ, cemittiya eḻuttuk kaḷiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟum, avai piṉīciya makkaḷāl ki.mu. 1000 varaiyil intiya nāṭṭiṟ parappappaṭṭateṉṟum karutappaṭṭaṉa. ikkaruttē nīṇṭakālam uṇmaiyeṉak karutappaṭṭu vantatu. tāma° kūṟiyataṟku aṟupatu āṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉ, pērāciriyar lāṅṭaṉ (Professor Langdon) eṉpār, pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷ cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyeṉak kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. intu nākarikam - irātā kumuta mukarji1.
tirāviṭar mattiyatarai nāṭukaḷiliruntu vantavarkaḷā?
tirāviṭa makkaḷ mattiyatarai nāṭukaḷiliruntu vantavarkaḷ eṉṟu nīṇṭakālam ārāycciyāḷarāl karutappaṭalāyiṟṟu. cintuveḷip putaiporuḷ ārāyccikkup piṉ hera° pātiriyār, ji.ār.haṇṭar, pērāciriyar, piṟāṅpōṭ, car.jāṉ.mārcal, cilṭi, ṭākṭar, hāl pōṉṟa ciṟanta ārāycci aṟiñarkaḷ, intiya nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟu mēṟku āciyāviṟ kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷē mattiyatarai makkaḷ āvar eṉat teḷivuṟak kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakattil carittira ārāyccip pulamai naṭattum tiru. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷum ikkaruttaiyē veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. ivvāṟu aṟiñar tuṇitaṟku ētuvāyuḷḷa kāraṇaṅkaḷai arāyntu pārātum, avarkaḷ kūṟṟukkaḷai maṟukkātum ārāycciyāḷar oruvar, intiyamakkaḷ mattiyatarai nāṭukaḷiliruntu vantavarkaḷāvar eṉa vāvā kūṟuvarēl avar kūṟṟu eṭṭuṇaiyum vali peṟātatākum. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ārāycci yeṉṉum nūl eḻutiyavar, cintuveḷi makkaḷ mattiyatarai nāṭukaḷi liruntu vantu kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ eṉṟum, avarkaḷiṉ nākarikam mattiya tarai nākarikam eṉṟum kūṟiyavai ciṟitum valipeṟāmai kāṇka.
mattiyatarai makkaḷ intiyarē
arappā mokañcatarō eḻuttukkaḷaiyum nākarikattaiyum naṉku ārāynta ṭākṭar ji.ār.haṇṭar kūṟuvatu varumāṟu:
1“āriyar varukaikku muṉ tirāviṭar cintu veḷikaḷil vāḻnta varkaḷ eṉpataṟku ēṟṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷ pulappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. palucci°tāṉattil pirākūyar vaḻaṅkum moḻi, tirāviṭattukku iṉamāyiruppatāl ivvuṇmai valiyuṟukiṉṟatu. 2itu vēṟu cāṉṟukaḷilum pārkkac ciṟappuṭaiyatu. haitarāpāttu, ceṉṉai mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kiḷaṟik kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa camātikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷil mokañca tarō, arappā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭavaikaḷai otta eḻuttukkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ivviṭaṅkaḷ tirāviṭamoḻikaḷukkuriya nāṭukaḷākum. tirāviṭar cintuvil ki.mu. 1100-kku muṉ vāḻntārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṭamiruntē cintu nākarikam avarkaḷ kaikku māṟiṟṟu. “āriyar mattiya āciyāvilē kāṭṭu vāḻkkaiyiṉarāyirunta kālattil intiya nāṭṭil vāḻnta tirāviṭar cemittiyarai otta nākarikamuṭaiyavarkaḷā yiruntaṉar. cālamaṉ, haram eṉum aracar im makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu vaittiruntatōṭu tirāviṭac coṟkaḷaiyum tamatu moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkiṉar. *cālamaṉ kālam ki.mu.1,000 varaiyil. ak kālattil iṉṟu vaḻaṅkum tamiḻccoṟkaḷ cila epirēya moḻiyil vaḻaṅkiṉavāyiṉ, caṅkakālat tamiḻ akkālattamiḻaiviṭa atika māṟutal aṭaintirunta teṉak kūṟa muṭiyātu. cālamaṉ kālattu vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ iṉṟaiyat tamiḻilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl mokañcatarō kālat tamiḻukkum caṅkakālat tamiḻukkumuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu, iṉṟaiyat tamiḻuk kum caṅkakālat tamiḻukku muḷḷa vēṟupāṭṭai ottatākalām eṉak karututal piḻaiyākātu. ivvuṇmaiyaic ciṟitum karuttiṟ koḷḷātu “tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaippaṟṟiya ārāycci eḻutiyavar caṅkakālat tamiḻ iṉṟaiya tamiḻilum pārkka vēṟāṉatu. hera° pātiriyār cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷukkut tamiḻccoṟkaḷaip peytu vācippatu takātu; immuṟai moḻiyārāycci muṟaiyāṉa vaḻikku māṟupaṭṭatu eṉak kuṟippiṭuvārāyiṉar. hera° pātiriyār kūṟṟukkaḷ eḷitil taḷḷiviṭat takkaṉavalla. avar tamiḻ aṟiyātavar. avar mokañcatarō eḻuttuk kaḷai olimuṟaiyāka vācittapōtu avvolikaḷ kuṟippiṭum coṟkaḷ, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil iruppatai avar piṟar vāyilākavē aṟintār. avar 1945il ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakattil pala virivuraikaḷ nikaḻttiṉār. appoḻutu avar mokañcatarō eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vācippukkaḷ. cilavaṟip pala paṭṭatārikaḷ, pērāciriyarkaḷ muṉṉilai yil kūṟiṉār. avar kūṟṟukkaḷai nērilō eḻuttumūlamō evarum maṟukkavillai. mauṉam uṭaṉpāṭṭiṉ aṭaiyāḷamēyākum. mēṟ paṭi tirāviṭamoḻikaḷiṉ ārāycci eḻutiyavarum, hera° pātiri yāriṉ virivuraikaḷaik kēṭṭu makiḻntavarkaḷuḷ oruvarāvar. iṉṟum pampāyil paḻañcarittira ārāycci naṭattivarum hera° pātiriyārai itu toṭarpāka viṉāvi avar uṇmai aṟiyalāmaṉṟō?
tirukkuṟaḷ, vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarppā?
tirukkuṟaḷiṉ pala pakutikaḷ cāṇakkiyar ceyta artta cāttirattiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭateṉac cilar kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. cāṇakkiyar kāñcipurattiṉiṉṟum vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟu mayūra cantirakuptaṉiṉ mantiriyāyirunta tamiḻar. 1ivar vaṭamoḻiyil artta cāttiram mutaliya pala nūlkaḷ ceytu pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉār. accuvittai kaṇṭupiṭik kappaṭṭu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa paṭikaḷ veḷivarum ik kālattilēyēpala nūlkaḷ namakkuk kiṭaikka villai. cāṇakkiyar ceytuḷḷa artta cāttira nūl teṉṉāṭṭut tiruvaḷḷuvarukkuk kiṭaittatu eṉa nampamuṭiyātu. teṉṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya cila ariya karuttukkaḷaiyē cāṇakkiyar vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ceytu pukaḻ aṭaintār. tiruvaḷḷuvarum teṉṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya atē karuttiṉai muṟaippaṭutti nūl ceytār. iruvarum tamiḻarātalālum, iruvarukkum nūl ceyvataṟku ātāram oṉṟu ātalālum, artta cāttiramum tirukkuṟaḷum pala iṭaṅkaḷil orē vakaiyākak kāṇappaṭutal viyappu aṉṟeṉṟu. āriyarukku nūlkaḷaic ceytu utaviya tamiḻarkaḷuḷ cāṇakkiyar1 oruvarāvar.
tirukkuṟaḷiṉ kālam
tirukkuṟaḷiṉ kālattiṉ kīḻ ellai ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉpataṟku uriya ātāram uḷḷatu. cilappatikāram ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil ceyyappaṭṭatu. cilappatikārattil tirukkuṟaḷaippaṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉāl tirukkuṟaḷ cilappatikārattukku muṟpaṭṭa teṉṟu tuṇiyappaṭukiṉṟatu.2
moḻinaṭai, collāṭci mutaliya cilavaṟṟai māttiram koṇṭu cilar tirukkuṟaḷ, cilappatikāram mutaliya nūlkaḷ, kālattāṟ piṟpaṭṭaṉa eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avar kūṟṟukkaḷ vali peṟumāṟillai. kālam kuṟippataṟkuriya pala cāṉṟukaḷōṭu collāṭci mutaliyaṉavum oru cāṉṟākumēyaṉṟi, avaikaḷaikkoṇṭu māttiram nūlkaḷiṉ kālattait tuṇintuviṭutal kūṭātu. iṉṟu āṉanta vikaṭaṉil eḻutap paṭum naṭaikkum karantait tamiḻccaṅkattāriṉ māta itaḻil eḻutappaṭum naṭaikkum ettuṇai vēṟupāṭu uḷatu. ivvēṟupāṭṭaik koṇṭu āṉanta vikaṭaṉum, tamiḻppoḻilum vevvēṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil uḷḷaṉa eṉak kūṟalāmā?
cilappatikārattiṉ piṟpakuti piṟarevarālō eḻutic cērkkap paṭṭateṉṉum ātāramaṟṟa koḷkai oṉṟum cila ārāycciyāḷarāl kiḷappappaṭukiṉṟatu. cilappatikārattiṉ piṟpakuti piṟarāl ceyyappaṭṭa teṉpataṟku ātāramillai. atil kūṟappaṭṭaṉa cila uṇmaiyallātaṉa pōl tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa veṉil, avai ak kālattai aṭuttirunta meka°tīṉa° pōṉṟa varalāṟ ṟāciriyarkaḷum, purāṇakārarkaḷum kūṟiya vaikaḷaiviṭa nampat takkaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉpatai nōkki uṇmai teḷital vēṇṭum.
cilappatikāra kālam
cilappatikārattil,
“āṭittiṅkaṭ pēriruṭ pakkat
taḻalcēr kuṭṭat taṭṭami ñāṉṟu
veḷḷi vārat toḷḷeri yuṇṇa
vuraicāṉ maturaiyō ṭracukē ṭuṟumeṉu
muṟaiyu muṇṭē”
eṉavarum cōtiṭak kuṟippiṉ kālam ki.mu.202 eṉa ṭākṭar kiruṣṇacāmi aiyaṅkārum, ki.pi.171 eṉa1 mu.ḻu. cēṣa aiyarum muṟaiyē kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. cēṣa aiyaravarkaḷ tamatu muṭivaik kūṟumuṉ, ṭākṭar kiruṣṇacāmi aiyaṅkār avarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷataiyum ārāyntu pārttuḷḷār. 11-m paripāṭaliṟ kāṇappaṭum cōtiṭak kuṟippiṉ kālam ki.pi.196-jūṉµ 28² (28th June 196 A.C.) eṉa mu.ḻu.caṅkar eṉpār kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ ellōrum caṅkakālattiṉ kīḻ ellai ki.pi, mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉṉum muṭivukku vantuḷḷārkaḷ. cilappati kāramum maṇimēkalaiyum caṅkakāla nūlkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cilappatikārañ ceytavar cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ tampiyākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kaṇṇakikku ālayam eṭuttu viḻāk koṇṭāṭiya kālattil pala nāṭṭu aracarkaḷ aṅkuc ceṉṟiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷuḷ ilaṅkaik kayavākum oruvaṉ. ilaṅkaik kayavāku ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil vāḻntavaṉ. ilaṅkaik kayavākuviṉ kālam aṟiyappaṭutaliṉāl, cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭu vaṉiṉ kālamum, avaṉ kālattukku muṉṉum piṉṉum irunta cila aracarkaḷiṉ kālaṅkaḷum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē cilappatikāram, tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, aracarkaḷ, pulavarkaḷ cilariṉ kālaṅkaḷai varaiyaṟuppataṟku ellaik kalpōṉṟu irukkiṉṟatu. cilappatikārattil tirukkuṟaḷ eṭuttu āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ākavē tirukkuṟaḷ cilappatikārat tukku muṟpaṭṭa nūl eṉat tuṇiyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. karikālaṉ kāviriyiṉ āṇaiyaik kaṭṭiyataippaṟṟip paṭṭiṉappālai kūṟukiṉṟatu. muṉpu cōḻa aracaṉ, kāviri aṇai kaṭṭuvataṟkuc ciṟaiyākap piṭittucceṉṟa ilaṅkai makkaḷaik kayavāku mīṭṭukkoṇṭu vantatōṭu, pattiṉiyiṉ cilampaiyum koṇṭuvantāṉ eṉṟu ciṅkaḷa nūlākiya irācāvaḷi kūṟukiṉṟatu. kayavāku pattiṉi viḻāvukkuc cēra nāṭu ceṉṟiruntāṉ eṉac cilappatikāram kūṟukiṉṟatu. ilaṅkai nūtaṉa poruṭkāṭcic cālaiyil ki.pi.āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil cetukkappaṭṭateṉṟu karutappaṭum kaṇṇakic cilai oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kaṇṇaki vaḻipāṭu ilaṅkai yil mikappaḻamaiyē vērūṉṟiyuḷḷateṉa pērāciriyar āṉantak kumāracāmi avarkaḷ viḷakkiyuḷḷār. ilaṅkai varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷ eṉṉum nūlil itaṉai car.pi.aruṇācalam avarkaḷum ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷār. ciṅkaḷavar viḻākkaḷil pattiṉic cilampu ūrvalattil eṭuttuc cellappaṭṭatu. ciṅkaḷa makkaḷ putta ālayaṅkaḷil vaittu vaḻipaṭṭa pattiṉic cilampukaḷ palavaṟṟai iṉṟum koḻumpu nūtaṉa poruṭkāṭcic cālaiyil kāṇalām. cilappatikārattaip paṟṟit tamiḻppulavarkaḷum piṟarum naṉku aṟiya vantatu atu acciṭappaṭṭa piṉpēyākum. iṟṟaikku nāṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ ilaṅkaiyil camarācaṉ eṉṉum pulavar oruvar kaṭalōṭṭukkātai eṉṉum nūl ceytār. annūl ituvaraiyil accēṟavivlai appulavar tām collum kaṭalōṭṭukkātai cilappatikārattai ātāramākak koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭateṉak kūṟukiṉṟār. āṉāl atil colvaṉa cilappati kārattil kāṇappaṭavillai. itaṉāl iṟṟaikku nāṉūṟu allatu ainnūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉum tamiḻppulavarkaḷ perumpālōr cilappatikārattaip paṟṟi peyaraḷavil aṟintiruntārkaḷ. eṉat terikiṉṟatu. ciṅkaḷattil irācāvaḷi ceytavarkaḷ cilappatikārattaip paṟṟik kēḷvippaṭṭirukka māṭṭārkaḷ. cilappatikārattaippaṟṟiyē aṟiyātār, kayavāku pattiṉic cilampaik koṇṭu vantāṉeṉṟum, kayavākuvukku muṉ ciṟaiyākap paṭikkappaṭṭa ciṅkaḷavar kāviri aṇai kaṭṭap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷavai, kayavāku pattiṉik kaṭavuḷiṉ viḻāvukku vantiruntāṉeṉṟum, avaṉ ilaṅkai yil pattiṉikkuk kōyil amaintāṉeṉṟum kūṟum varalāṟṟai ottuḷḷaṉa. ākavē iv voṟṟumaikaḷai ātāramaṟṟaṉa eṉṟu taḷḷi viṭutal muṭiyātu.
S.K. cēṣa aiyaravarkaḷ caṅkakālac cēranāṭu eṉṉum nūlil cilappatikāra kālattaip paṟṟi ārāyntu kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu:
paḻaṅkālap pulavarkaḷ tām niṉaittapaṭi cōtiṭak kuṟippuk kaḷaik kūṟiṉārkaḷ eṉa nām karututal tavaṟu. maturaiyait tī uṇṭatākac cilappatikāram kūṟumkālam. ki.pi.171-m āṇṭu aṭiµ veḷḷivāram irupattāṟāvatu nāḷākum. ak kālattil ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vāḻntāṉ. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālattil kaṭal vāṇikam ucca nilai aṭaintiruntatu. itu piḷiṉi eṉpavar (ki.pi.161-maraṇam) muciṟiyaip paṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷāl naṉkuviḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kaṭalōṭṭiya vēl ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. intiyac carittirattil kālaṅkaḷaik kuṟippiṭuvataṟkuc cilappatikāram ellaik kallāka virukkiṉṟatu. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭanāṭṭu aracar mītu paṭai eṭuttāṉ eṉac collap paṭukiṉṟatu. avaṉ kaṅkaik karaiyil pōr ceytapōtu nūṟṟuvarkaṉṉar avaṉukku utaviyaḷittaṉar. avaṉai etirttavarkaḷuḷ, vicayar, pāla kumārar, uruttirar mutaliya peyarkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avarkaḷai veṉṟapiṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ aṅkiruntu pattiṉic cilai ceyvataṟku oru kallai eṭuttuvantāṉ. kalnāṭṭiyapōtu pala nāṭṭu aracar vantirun tārkaḷ. avarkaḷuḷ ilaṅkaik kayavāku aracaṉ oruvaṉāvaṉ. ceṅ kuṭṭuvaṉallāta aracar kālattil vaṭanāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭai eṭukka iyalātavāṟu teṉṉintiya aracar palam kuṉṟiyiruntaṉar. vaṭaintiya varalāṟṟai nōkkumpōtu, iraṇṭumūṉṟu muṟai māttiram teṉ ṉintiyar vaṭakku nōkkip paṭai eṭukkat takkatāyintatu. ki.mu.mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil cantirakuptaṉ pimpicāraṉ acōkaṉ mutaliyōr kālattil teṉṉāṭṭār vaṭakkē paṭai eṭuttirukkamuṭiyātu. puṣya mittiraṉ kālattilum itu nikaḻntirukkamuṭiyātu. puṣya mittiraṉ ki.mu.234-kkum ki.mu.184-kku miṭaiyil āṭci purintāṉ. cuṅka aracariṉ piṟkāla āṭci kuḻappamaṭaintiruntatu cātavākaṉar allatu cartakarīkayar atikāram eytamuyaṉṟu koṇṭiruntārkaḷ. ki.pi. mutalām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil avarkaḷ kaṇṇuvariṉ āṭciyaik kaippaṟṟiṉārkaḷ. nācik paṭṭaiyattil srīcātakarṇiyiṉ peyar kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. puṣyamittira ṉiṉ maraṇattukkuppiṉ cuṅkarum kaṇṇuvarum kuḻappam aṭaintirunta kālattil oru paṭai eṭuppukku iṭam iruntatu. itu ki.mu.148-kkum ki.mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭukkum iṭaiyil. kautami puttira srīcātakarṇikkuppiṉ (ki.pi.109-135) pulamāy eṉpavaṉ 35 āṇṭukaḷ āṭcipurintāṉ. avaṉ, mutalām uruttiratāmaṉōṭu mōtik koṇṭāṉ. uruttiratāmaṉ kautami puttira srīcātakarṇi cākaratariṭamiruntu veṉṟa nāṭukaḷaip piṭuṅkikkoṇṭāṉ. āṉāl kautami puttiraṉ yāññasrī (ki.pi.173-202) pulamāy iḻanta cila nāṭukaḷai mīṭṭukkoṇṭāṉ, cātavākaṉar akkālattil teṉṉāṭṭup paṭaiyeṭuppai etirkkak kūṭātavāṟu palavīṉamaṭaintiruntārkaḷ. yāñña srīyiṉ kālattukkuppiṉ iruṭ kālam eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. piṉpu mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭai aṭaikiṉṟōm. nāṉkām aintām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākiya kuptarkaḷ kālattaippaṟṟi naṉṟākat terikiṉṟatu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭu vākaṭariṉ kālamākum. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil atikāram paṭaitta pallava cāḷukkiya kālattukku varukiṉ ṟōm. cāḷukkiyar ki.pi.753il irāṣṭira kuptarāl vīḻttappaṭṭārkaḷ. nālām nuṟṟāṇṭu mutal aṭutta nāṉūṟu āṇṭu varaiyil vaṭanāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭai eṭuppataṟku ēṟṟa kālamāka irukkavillai.
maturai ki.pi.171il tīppaṭṭatāyiṉum, appōtu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ āṭcipurintukoṇṭiruntāṉāyiṉum, kaṇṇaki vāṉulakaṭaintapiṉ, nūṟṟuvarkaṉṉar utavi koṭuppatāka uṟuti kūṟiyapiṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭanāṭṭiṉmītu paṭai eṭuttāṉākavēṇṭum, nūṟṟuvarkaṉṉar eṉpōr cātakarṇikaḷē. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ taṉatu nāṭṭaiviṭṭu 32 tiṅkaḷ veḷiyē ceṉṟiruntāṉ. appoḻutu avaṉ kaṅkaik karaiyiliruntāṉ. avaṉ kaṅkaikkaraiyilirunta kālam ki.pi.175-ṉ muṟpakuti eṉak kūṟalām. tamatu muṉṉōṉāṉa pulumāy iḻanta nāṭukaḷaic cātakarṇikaḷ peṟuvataṟkuc cātakarṇikaḷ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ utaviyai vēṇṭiyirukkalām. itaṉāl ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ yāññasrīyiṉ kālattavaṉ eṉat terikiṉṟatu. itaṉāl nām ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ vaṭanāṭṭup paṭaiyeṭuppaippaṟṟi naṉku aṟiyakkūṭiyatāyirukkiṉṟatu. cātakarṇikaḷ tamatu nōkkam niṟaivēṟiyapōtu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ viruppaiyum niṟai vēṟṟiṉārkaḷ. pala āriya aracar ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉai etirttārkaḷ eṉṟum, avarkaḷil oruvaṉ pālkumāraṉiṉ makaṉ vicayaṉ eṉavum paṭukiṉṟāṉ. pālakumāraṉ eṉpavaṉ tālimi kūṟum pāliyō kurū°. (Baleokouros,) cila varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ pāliyōkurū° cātavākaṉa aracaruḷ oruvaṉeṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivaṉuṭaiya makaṉē ceṅ kuṭṭuvaṉaik kaṅkaikkaraiyil etirttavaṉākalām. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉāl tōṟkaṭikkappaṭṭa iṉṉoruvaṉ uruttiraṉ. ivaṉ uruttiracaṉmaṉ allatu uruttiracēṉaṉākalām. ivaṉiṭatti liruntē ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ cātavākaṉar iḻnta nāṭṭaik kaippaṟṟiṉāṉ. kaṅkaip pōr eṉpatu yāññasrīyālum avaṉ tuṇaivarkaḷālum vaṭanāṭṭu aracarukku etirāka naṭanta pōrākum.
kaṇṇakikkuk kalnāṭṭiyapōtu vantirunta aracaruḷ ilaṅkaik kayavāku oruvaṉ eṉac cilappatikāram kūṟukiṉṟatu. makāvamicattiṉpaṭi kayavākuviṉ āṭcik kālam ki.pi.173-mutal 191varai. irācāvaḷi eṉṉum ciṅkaḷa nūl, kayavāku pattiṉik kaṭavuḷiṉ cilampai eṭuttuc ceṉṟāṉ eṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu. kayavāku pattiṉik kaṭavuḷukkuk kōyil eṭuttāṉ eṉṉum varalāṟṟōṭu itu ottirukkiṉṟatu. pattiṉikkaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu ilaṅkaiyil paḻaṅkāla mutal uḷḷatu eṉṟu āṉantak kumāracāmi avarkaḷ viḷakkiyuḷḷār - (J.R.A.S.1909 p22).
maturai ki.pi.171-l tīppaṭṭatu eṉṉum koḷkai palavakaiyāl uṟutippaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ kālam iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉak kūṟuvatu poruttamāyirukkiṉṟatu.
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉum kayavākuvum orēkālattavarkaḷ eṉṉum koḷkai eḷitil taḷḷiviṭakkūṭiyataṉṟu. cilappatikāram makāvamicam mutaliya nūlkaḷ varalāṟṟut toṭarpāṉavai.

nīti neṟi viḷakkam
mūlamum uraiyum
āṅkila moḻi peyarppum
muṉṉurai
piṟkāla nūlkaḷuḷ nītineṟi viḷakkam col naṭai poruṭ ceṟivu mutaliyavaikaḷāl ciṟappamaintatu. ak kālattil maturaiyai āṇṭa tirumalai nāyakaṉ (1623-1659) eṉṉum aracaṉiṉ vēṇṭu kōḷukku icaintu kumarakuruparar nītineṟi viḷakkam eṉṉum nūlait tirukkuṟaḷiṉ cāramākac ceytāreṉpatu paramparai varalāṟu. mēlai nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum kiṟittuva matattaip parappum poruṭṭu intiya nāṭu pōntār tamatu koḷkaikaḷaip parappum poruṭṭu makkaḷ pēcum moḻikaḷaip payilum kaṭṭāyam uṇṭāyiruntatu. tamiḻaip payilat toṭaṅkiya mēl nāṭṭavar tamiḻil uḷḷa tirukkuṟaḷ eṉṉum oppuyar villāta nūlaik kaṇṭu viyappaṭaintu ataṉaik kaṟṟu ataṉait tattam moḻikaḷil moḻi peyarttukkoṇṭaṉar. tirukkuṟaḷai oppa mēlnāṭṭavariṉ kavaṉattai īrtta iṉṉoru nūl nītineṟi viḷakkam. in nūlai sṭōks (H. Stokes, I.C.S) 1830il accēṟṟi veḷiyiṭ ṭuḷḷār. vēṟu palar itaṉai āṅkilattil moḻi peyarttuḷḷārkaḷ. ip patippu putiya teḷḷiya uraiyōṭum āṅkila moḻipeyarppōṭum kūṭiyatu.
kumarakuruparar
nītineṟi viḷakkam eṉṉum ariya nīti nūlai aruḷic ceyta kumarakurupara cuvāmikaḷ 1675ām āṇṭu (ciṟī vaikuntattiṟ piṟantār. piṟantu aintu āṇṭaḷavum ivar ūmai yāyiruntār. piṉpu ivar pēcum vaṉmai aṭaintār. ivar teṉṉintiyāviṟ pala iṭaṅkaḷukkut tala yāttirai ceyta piṉ 1658il kācikkuc ceṉṟār. aṅku ivar mukamatiya aracaṉāl naṉku matikkappaṭṭu avaṉāl aḷikkappaṭṭa nilattil maṭam oṉṟaik kaṭṭiṉār. 1988il ivar kālamāṉār. ivaruṭaiya pāṭalkaḷ coṟcuvai poruṭcuvai nirampiyavai. ivar ceyta nūlkaḷ 1. kantar kaliveṇpā, 2. mīṉāṭci piḷḷait tamiḻ, 3. mīṉāṭci kuṟam, 4. mīṉāṭci iraṭṭai maṇimālai, 5. paṇṭāra mummaṇik kōvai, 6. muttukkumāra cuvāmi piḷḷait tamiḻ, 7. citampara mummaṇik kōvai, 8. nītineṟi viḷakkam, 9. maturaik kalampakam, 10. kācik kalampakam, 11. citamparac ceyyuṭ kōvai eṉpaṉavām.
ceṉṉai
1.4.1949 na.ci. kantaiyā
nīti neṟi viḷakkam
mūlamum uraiyum
āṅkila moḻipeyarppum
kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu
nīriṟ kumiḻi iḷamai niṟaicelvam
nīriṟ curuṭṭu neṭuntiraikaḷ - nīril
eḻuttākum yākkai namaraṅkāḷ eṉṉē
vaḻuttāta tempirāṉ maṉṟu. 1
1. Youth is a bubble on the water: riches in full abundance are the long waves that roll on the water; the body is a writing on the water. Why, O my friends, worship we not the Court of our God?
(patavurai) iḷamai nīril kumiḻi - iḷamaip paruvam nīril kumiḻi pōṉṟu nilai illātatu; niṟai celvam nīril curuṭṭu neṭum tiraikaḷ - niṟainta celvam (kaṭal) nīrilē curuṇṭu varukiṉṟa nīṇṭa alaikaḷ pōṉṟu tōṉṟi maṟaivatu; yākkai nīril eḻuttākum - uṭampu taṇṇīril eḻutiya eḻuttup pōl viraivil maṟaintu pōvatu; (ātaliṉ) namaraṅkāḷ - nammavarē! empirāṉ maṉṟu vaḻuttātatu eṉ - ematu kaṭavuḷiṉ capaiyait tutiyātatu ēṉō?
(karutturai) uṭampu nilaiyaṟṟatu. ātalāl atu nilait tirukkum pōtē kaṭavuḷai vaḻipaṭal vēṇṭum.
kalviyē uṟutiyāṉa tuṇai
aṟamporu ḷiṉpamum vīṭum payakkum
puṟaṅkaṭai nallicaiyum nāṭṭum - uṟuṅkavaloṉ
ṟuṟṟuḻiyuṅ kaikoṭukkuṅ kalviyi ṉūṅkillai
ciṟṟuyirk kuṟṟa tuṇai. 2
2. It will bestow virtue, riches, pleasure and Heaven. It will establish a good report in the world, and when any great affliction may betide, it will afford a hand; short-lived mortals have no surer stay than learning.
(pa-u) (kalvi) aṟamporuḷ iṉpamum vīṭum payakkum - (kalvi) aṟattaiyum poruḷaiyum iṉpattaiyum vīṭu pēṟṟaiyum koṭukkum; puṟam kaṭai nal icaiyum nāṭṭum - ulakil nalla pukaḻaiyum nilai niṟuttum; uṟum kaval oṉṟu uṟṟuḻiyum kai koṭukkum - periya tuṉpam oṉṟu nērntaviṭattum tuṇai ceyyum; (ātaliṉ) ciṟu uyirkku uṟṟa tuṇai kalviyiṉ ūṅku illai - ākavē ciṟiya vāḻnāḷuṭaiya makkaḷukku uṟutiyākiya tuṇai kalviyilum mēlāka vēṟoṉṟillai.
puṟaṅkaṭai - puṟattē.
(ka-u) kalvi makkaḷukku ellāp payaṉum koṭuttu utavattakkatu. ātalāl ataṉaik kaṟṟal vēṇṭum.
kalvi kaṟpatu tuṉpamāyiṉum atu piṉ iṉpantarum
toṭaṅkuṅkāl tuṉpamāy iṉpam payakkum
maṭaṅkoṉ ṟaṟivakaṟṟuṅ kalvi - neṭuṅkāmam
muṟpayakkuñ ciṉṉīra viṉpattiṉ muṟṟiḻāy
piṟpayakkum pīḻai peritu. 3
3. O thou adoned with perfect jewels! Learning at first painful, will afterwards afford pleasure. It will destroy ignorance and extend knowledge. But the pain, which succeeds to the short-lived pleasure of immoderate lust,is great. 
(pa-u) muṟṟu iḻāy - muṟṟāka muṭinta vēlaippāṭukaḷuṭaiya nakaikaḷai aṇintavaṉē; maṭam koṉṟu aṟivu akaṟṟum kalvi - aṟiyāmaiyaik keṭuttu aṟivai vicālappaṭuttum kalvi; toṭaṅkuṅ kāl tuṉpamāy iṉpam payakkum - kaṟkat toṭaṅkum pōtu tuṉpam taruvatākip piṉpu makiḻcciyaik koṭukkum; neṭum kāmam - mikunta kāmam; muṟpayakkum ciṉnīra iṉpattil piṟpayakkum piḻai peritu - mutalil koṭukkum ciṟitu nēra iṉpattilum pārkkap piṉpu koṭukkum tuṉpam peritākum.
(ka-u) kalvi toṭakkattil tuṉpamākat tōṉṟippiṉ iṉpan tarum; kāmam mutalil iṉpamākat tōṉṟippiṉ tuṉpantarum.
capaiyai alaṅkarikkum kalvi
kalviyē kaṟpuṭaip peṇṭirap peṇṭirkkuc
celvap putalvaṉē tīṅkaviyāc - colvaḷam
mallal veṟukkaiyā māṇavai maṇṇuṟuttuñ
celvamu muṇṭu cilarkku. 4
4. Blest with learning as a chaste spouse, with sweet poctry as the darling son of that spouse, and with fluency of language as a store of riches, there are some whose wealth adorns the illustrious assembly of the learned.
(pa-u) cilarkku kalviyē kaṟpuṭaip peṇṭir (ā) - kalvi kaṟkum cilarkkuk kalviyē kaṟpuṭaiya maṉaiviyākavum; appeṇṭirkkut tīm kaviyē celvap putalvaṉ ā - am maṉaivikku iṉiya pāṭalē celvap putalvaṉākavum; colvaḷam mallal veṟukkai ā - collum tiṟamaiyē niṟainta celvamākavum; māṇ avai maṇṇuṟuttum celvamum uṇṭu - periya aṟivuṭaiyōr capaiyai alaṅkarikkiṉṟa celvamum uṇṭu.
(ka-u) cilarukku māttiram nuliyaṟṟumāṟṟalōṭu ciṟanta pēcumāṟṟalum uṇṭu.
kalvikkuc ciṟappu colvaṉmai
ettuṇaiya vāyiṉuṅ kalvi iṭamaṟin
tuyttuṇar villeṉiṉ illākum - uyttuṇarntum
colvaṉmai yiṉṟeṉiṉ eṉṉākum aḵtuṇṭēl
poṉmalar nāṟṟa muṭaittu. 5
5. Learning, however extensive, will be useless, unless there be discretion to display it in the proper place; and even then, without the power of language, of what avail is it? With that, it is a flower of gold that possesses fragrance.
(pa-u) kalvi ettuṇaiya āyiṉum - (oruvaṉukkuk) kalvi evvaḷavu irunta pōtum; iṭam aṟintu uyttu uṇarvu il eṉil illākum - nūliṉ iṭattai aṟintu ārāyntu aṟiyum aṟivu illaiyāyiṉ atu payaṉ paṭātākum; uyttuṇarntum - avvāṟu ārāyntu aṟintum; colvaṉmai iṉṟu eṉiṉ eṉṉākum - pēcum āṟṟal illaiyāyiṉ eṉṉa payaṉuṇṭākum? aḵtu uṇṭēl poṉ malar nāṟṟam uṭaittu - pēccuvaṉmai iruntāl poṉ malar maṇam peṟṟatu pōṉṟatākum.
(ka-u) iṭamaṟintu poruḷaṟitalum pēccu vaṉmaiyum vēṇṭum.
avai añcuvār kalvi ciṟantataṉṟu
avaiyañci meyvitirppār kalviyum kallār
avaiyañcā ākulac collum - navaiyañci
īttuṇṇār celvamum nalkūrntā riṉṉalamum
pūttaliṟ pūvāmai naṉṟu. 6
6. The learning of those whose frame trembles with diffidence before the assembly - the frivolous loquacity of the ignorant who feel no awe in the assembly - the wealth of those who do not conscientiously bestow alms before they eat - and the merit of a poor man, are things, the absence of which is better than their existance.
(pa-u) avai añci mey vitirppār kalviyum - capaikkup payantu uṭampu naṭuṅkukiṉṟavariṉ kalviyum; kallār - paṭiyātavariṉ; avai añcā ākulac collum - capaikkup payappaṭāta āravārac collum; navai añci īttu uṇṇār celvamum - kuṟṟattukku añci (vaṟiya varkkuk) koṭuttuṇṇātavariṉ celvamum; nalkūrntār iṉ nalamum - vaṟiyavariṉ iṉiya kuṇaṅkaḷum; pūttalil pūvāmai naṉṟu - tōṉṟutalil tōṉṟāmai nallatu.
(ka-u) capaikku añcum kalviyaṟivāl payaṉillai.
ūṉuṭampilum pukaḻuṭampē mēlāṉatu
kalaimakaḷ vāḻkkai mukatta teṉiṉum
malaravaṉ vaṇṭamiḻōrk kovvāṉ - malaravaṉcey
veṟṟuṭampu māyvaṉapōl māyā pukaḻkoṇṭu
maṟṟivar ceyyum uṭampu. 7
7. Though Saraswati dwell in the face of both, Brahma may not vie with the illustrious Tamil bards; for the bodies, which these form by their praises, will not perish like the empty bodies of Brahma’s creation.
(pa-u) kalaimakaḷ vāḻkkai mukattatu eṉiṉum - nāmakaḷiṉ vāḻkkai (piramaṉatu) mukattilum (kaṟṟavar) mukattilumāṉālum; malaravaṉ vaṇ tamiḻōrkku ovvāṉ - piramā pukaḻuṭaiya tamiḻp pulavarukku oppāka māṭṭāṉ; (ēṉeṉṟāl) malaravaṉ cey veṟṟu uṭampu māyvaṉa pōl - piramā ceykiṉṟa veṟiya (pukaḻillāta) uṭampu aḻivataip pōla; ivar pukaḻkoṇṭu ceyyum maṟṟu uṭampu māyā - ippulavar pukaḻaik koṇṭu ceykiṉṟa vēṟu vakaiyāṉa uṭampu aḻiya māṭṭātu.
(ka-u) kalviyāluṇṭākum pukaḻ eṉṟum aḻiyātu.
capaiyil utavāk kalvi
neṭumpakaltām kaṟṟa avaiyat tutavā
tuṭaintuḷā ruṭkuvaruṅ kalvi - kaṭumpakal
ētilāṉ pāṟkaṇṭa illiṉum pollātē
tīteṉṟu nīppari tāl. 8
8. The shrinking learning of men broken by long study, which is not available in public, is worse than a wife seen in broad day-light in a neighbour’s, company, for it is an evil that cannot be put away.
(pa-u) neṭum pakal tām kaṟṟa - nīṇṭakālam tām paṭittavaikaḷai; avaiyattu utavātu - capaiyil eṭutturaikka muṭiyāmal; uṭaintu uḷār - maṉam varunti irukkiṉṟavarkaḷatu; uṭkuvarum kalvi - accattaik kāṭṭum kalvi; kaṭum pakal-naṭuppakalil; ētilāṉpāl kaṇṭa illiṉum pollātu - ayalavaṉiṭattil kaṇṭa oruvaṉuṭaiya maṉaiviyilum pārkkat tīyatākum; (ēṉeṉil) tītu eṉṟu nīppu aritu āl - (itu) tīyateṉṟu vilakki viṭutaṟku muṭiyātiruppatāl.
(ka-u) capaiyiṭattu utavāta kalviyāṟ payaṉillai.
paṭittavaṟṟai maṟantu putiyavaṟṟaik kaṟṟal
varuntittāṅ kaṟṟaṉa vōmpātu maṟṟum
parintucila kaṟpāṉ toṭaṅkal - karuntaṉam
kaittalatta uyttuc corintiṭ ṭaripparittāṅ
keyttup poruḷcey tiṭal. 9
9. The attempt of those who, without preserving that learning which they have acquired with difficulty, try to make fresh acquirements, is like throwing away gold from their hands and toiling to make a fortune by sifting dust in search of treasure.
(pa-u) varunti tām kaṟṟaṉa ōmpātu - muyaṉṟu tām paṭittavaikaḷai (niṉaivil vaittup) pātukāvāmal; maṟṟum cila parintu kaṟpāṉ toṭaṅkal - vēṟu cilavaṟṟai varuntik kaṟkat toṭaṅkutal; kai talatta karuntaṉam uyttu corintiṭṭu - (oruvaṉ) kaiyiṭattuḷḷa poṉṉai (nilattil) vīci eṟintuviṭṭu; āṅku - avviṭattilē; arippu arittu eyttup poruḷ ceytiṭal - aripparittu varuntip poruḷaic cērppatu pōlākum.
(ka-u) paṭittavaṟṟai maṟantu putiyavaṟṟaik kaṟkat toṭaṅkutal aṟiyāmai.
vaṟiyār kalvi viḷakkamaṭaiyātu
eṉaittuṇaiya vēṉum ilampaṭṭār kalvi
tiṉaittuṇaiyuñ cīrpā ṭilavām - maṉaittakkāḷ
māṇpila ḷāyiṉ maṇamakaṉ nallaṟam
pūṇṭa pulappaṭā pōl. 10
10. However great may be the learning of poor men, it will never be appreciated so much as a grain to tinay even as, if the mistress of the house possess not the qualifications which become a wife, the virtues, with which her husband is endued, will not become conspicuous.
(pa-u) maṉaittakkāḷ māṇpu ilaḷ āyiṉ - maṉaivi ciṟappillātavaḷāyiṉ; maṇamakaṉ pūṇṭa nal aṟam pulappaṭā. kaṇavaṉ koṇṭa illaṟam viḷakkamaṭaiya māṭṭātu; pōl - atu pōla; ilam paṭṭār kalvi eṉaittuṇaiya vēṉum - vaṟiyavaruṭaiya kalvi evvaḷavu peritāyiruntapōtum; tiṉai tuṇaiyum cīrpāṭu ilavām - tiṉaiyaḷavum ciṟappu aṭaital illaiyākum.
(ka-u) vaṟiyavar kalvi pirakācippatillai.
vaṟiyavar nallavarāyiṉum matippaṭaiyār
iṉcollaṉ tāḻnaṭaiya ṉuyiṉumoṉ ṟillāṉēl
vaṉcolli ṉallatu vāytiṟavā - eṉcoliṉum
kaittuṭaiyāṉ kāṟkīḻ otuṅkuṅ kaṭaṉñālam
pittuṭaiya valla piṟa. 11
11. Though he may be courteous in speech, and humble in his behaviour, they will not open their lips to a poor man except in harsh words. Whatever a rich man may say they will crouch beneath his feet. Surely this world surrounded by the sea is full of madness.
(pa-u) iṉ collaṉ - (oruvaṉ) iṉiyacoṟ pēcukiṉṟavaṉum; tāḻnaṭaiyaṉ āyiṉum - aṭakkamāṉa oḻukkamuṭaiyavaṉumāyiṉum; oṉ ṟillāṉēl - avaṉ ciṟitu poruḷāvatu illāmal iruppāṉāṉāl; vaṉcolliṉ allatu - (ulakattavar avaṉiṭattu) kaṭiya coṟkaḷaip pēcutalallatu; vāy tiṟavā - (iṉiya coṟkaḷaip) pēcamāṭṭār; kaittu uṭaiyāṉ eṉ coliṉum - kaiyiṟ poruḷuṭaiyavaṉ etaic coṉṉālum; kāl kīḻ otuṅkum - avaṉuṭaiya kāliṉ kīḻ aṭaṅkuvar; kaṭal ñālam pittuṭaiyatu - kaṭalcūḻnta ivvulakiluḷḷavarkaḷ paittiyam koṇṭavarāvar; piṟa alla - vēṟoṉṟum uṭaiyavarallar.
(ka-u) vaṟiyavar nallavarāyiṉum matippeytār; celvar tīyavarāyiṉum matippaṭaivar.
celvaraip palar cūḻntu kuṟaiyirappar
ivaṟaṉmai kaṇṭum uṭaiyārai yārum
kuṟaiyirantum kuṟṟēval ceypa - perituntām
muṟpakal nōlātār nōṟṟāraip piṉcellal
kaṟpaṉṟē kallāmai yaṉṟu. 12
12. Although they see their covetousness, all urge their wants on the rich, and perform their mean behests. The submission of those who were not eminently devout in a former birth to those who were, is doubtless not from ignorance, but the decree of destiny.
(pa-u) ivaṟaṉmai kaṇṭum - īyāta taṉmaiyai aṟintiruntum; yārum uṭaiyārai kuṟai irantum kuṟṟēval ceypa - ellārum poruḷuṭaiyavariṭam (ceṉṟu) tamatu kuṟaiyai nīkkumpaṭi kēṭṭu (avar) paṇiviṭaikaḷaiyum ceyvar; muṉ pakal tām peritum nōlātār - muṟpiṟappil tām peritum tavam ceyyātavar; nōṟṟārai piṉ cellal kaṟpu aṉṟē - tavam ceytavaraip piṉ ceṉṟu vēṇṭutal aṟivākum; kallāmai aṉṟu - (aḵtu) aṟiyāmai aṉṟu; ē - tēṟṟam.
(ka-u) poruḷ koṭāta ulōpiyarāyirunta pōtilum celvaraip palar cūḻntu kuṟaikaḷaic colli paṇiviṭai ceyvar.
kaṟṟārkkuk kalviyē aḻaku
kaṟṟōrkkuk kalvi nalaṉē kalaṉallāl
maṟṟōraṇikalam vēṇṭāvām - muṟṟa
muḻumaṇip pūṇukkup pūṇvēṇṭā yārē
aḻakuk kaḻaku ceyvār 13
13. The learned need no other ornament than the excellence of learning. Nothing is wanting to adornan ornament set perfectly with every precious stone. Who would beautify beauty itself?
(pa-u) muṟṟa muḻumaṇi pūṇukku pūṇvēṇṭā - (vēlaippāṭu) muṭivuṟumpaṭi muḻu māṇikkaṅkaḷ patitta āparaṇattukku aḻaku ceyvataṟku vēṟu āparaṇam vēṇṭiyatillai; (atupōla) kaṟṟōrkku kalvi nalaṉē kalaṉ allāl - paṭittavarkaḷukkup paṭippiṉ aḻakē aḻakāvatallatu; maṟṟu ōr aṇikalam vēṇṭāvām - (avarukku) vēṟu āparaṇam vēṇṭiyatillai; yārē aḻakukku aḻaku ceyvār - ēṉeṉil evar tām aḻakukku aḻaku ceyvar? (ceyyār eṉṟavāṟu)
(ka-u) kaṟṟavarukkuc ciṟanta aḻakāvatu kalviyē; piṟar matippeytuvataṟkuk kalviyillātavaraip pōla avar tammai alaṅkarittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatillai.
ellāmaṟintavar ulakil illai
muṟṟu muṇarntava rillai muḻuvatūuṅ
kaṟṟaṉa meṉṟu kaḷiyaṟka - ciṟṟuḷiyāṟ
kallun takarun takarā kaṉaṅkuḻāy
kollulaik kūṭattiṉāl. 14
14. There are none who understand every thing. Exult not in the idea that your learning is universal. The rock will give way to the small chisel of the mason; it will not, O thou adorned with massive ornaments, yield to the blacksmith’s hammer.
(pa-u) muṟṟum uṇarntavar illai - ellām aṟintavar (ivvulakattil) illai; mutuvatūum kaṟṟaṉam eṉṟu kaḷiyaṟka - (ākavē) ellāvaṟṟaiyum paṭittaṟintu viṭṭōmeṉṟu cerukkuk koḷḷa vēṇṭām; kaṉam kuḻāy - pāramāṉa kātaṇiyai aṇintavaḷē; ciṟu uḷiyāl kallum takarum - ciṟiya uḷiyiṉāl pāṟaikaḷum uṭaiyum; kol ulaik kūṭattiṉāl takarā - (āṉāl) kollaṉatu ulaiyiṭat tuḷḷa cammaṭṭiyiṉāl appāṟai uṭaiyātu.
(ka-u) ulakil ellām aṟintavar illai; periya kalvi āḷar aṟiyāta karuttukkaḷ cila potumakkaḷiṭattum kāṇappaṭum; ākavē kalvic cerukkuk kūṭātu.
kaṟṟārai nōkkic ceruk koḻika
tammiṉ meliyārai nōkkit tamatuṭaimai
ammā periteṉ ṟakamakiḻka - tammiṉum
kaṟṟārai nōkkik karuttaḻika kaṟṟatellām
eṟṟē ivarkku nām eṉṟu. 15
15. Contemplate those who are poorer than yourselves, and rejoice in the greatness of your possessions. Contemplate those who are more learned than yourselves, and destroy self-conceit, exclaiming what is all our learning to these!
(pa-u) tammiṉ meliyārai nōkki - tammaik kāṭṭilum vaṟiyavarkaḷaip pārttu; tamatu uṭaimai ammā peritu eṉṟu akam makiḻka - ammā! tammiṭattiluḷḷatu periyatu eṉṟu maṉam makiḻtal vēṇṭum; tammiṉum kaṟṟārai nōkki - tammilum pārkkap paṭittavaraip pārttu; nām kaṟṟatu ellām ivarkku eṟṟē eṉṟu karuttu aḻika - nām paṭittatellām ivar paṭippukku evvaḷaveṉṟu (cerukkāṉa) eṇṇattai viṭṭu viṭutal vēṇṭum.
(ka-u) tammilum vaṟiyavaraik kāṇum pōtu tamatu celvam peritu eṉṟu makiḻa vēṇṭum; tammilum kaṟṟāraik kāṇum pōtu tamatu kalvi aṟpam eṉṟu cerukku oḻiya vēṇṭum.
celvattuk kaḻaku paṇivu
kalvi yuṭaimai poruḷuṭaimai yeṉṟiraṇṭu
celvamuñ celva meṉappaṭum - illār
kuṟaiyirantu tammuṉṉar niṟpapōṟ ṟāmun
talaivaṇaṅkit tāḻap peṟiṉ. 16
16. The wealth, which consists in the possession of learning, and the possession of riches. may be termed wealth indeed, if its possessors bow the head with humility, as the destitute who stand begging before them.
(pa-u) illār-vaṟiyavar; kuṟai irantu tam muṉṉar niṟpapōl - tamatu kuṟaiyai vēṇṭit tamakku muṉṉāl niṟpatu pōla; tāmum talai vaṇaṅkit tāḻap peṟiṉ - tāṅkaḷum talaiyāl vaṇaṅkit tāḻntu niṟpārkaḷāyiṉ; kalvi uṭaimai poruḷ uṭaimai eṉṟu iraṇṭu celvamum - kalvic celvam poruṭcelvam eṉṉum iraṇṭu celvaṅkaḷum; celvam eṉappaṭum - ciṟanta celvaṅkaḷ eṉṟu (aṟivuṭaiyōrāl) collappaṭum.
(ka-u) kalvic celvattukkum poruṭ celvattukkum ciṟapput taruvatu paṇivē.
aṟivillātārukku aṟiñar tāḻntu naṭappar
ākkam periyār ciṟiyā riṭaippaṭṭa
mīccelavu kāṇiṉ naṉitāḻpa - tūkkiṉ
meliyatu mēṉmē leḻaccellac cella
valitaṉṟē tāḻun tulai. 17
17. If men of real dignity perceive presumption in the conduct of their inferiors, they will behave with more humility. In weighing, while the lighter rises higher and higher, does not the heavier sink down the scale?
(pa-u) tūkkiṉ - niṟuttup pārkkumpōtu; tulai meliyatu mēṉmēl eḻa cellac cella - tarācil pāraṅ kuṟainta poruḷuḷḷa taṭṭu mēlum mēlum uyarac cellac cella; valitu aṉṟē tāḻum - pāramāṉa poruḷuḷḷataṉṟō tāḻum; (atu pōla) ākkam periyār - aṟivālum celvattālum periyavar; ciṟiyōr iṭaipaṭṭa mī celavu kāṇiṉ naṉitāḻpa - (avviraṇṭumillāta) ciṟiyavariṭattu varampukaṭanta naṭattai kaṇṭāl avarkaḷukku mikavum tāḻntu naṭappar.
(ka-u) aṟivillātavar varampu kaṭantu naṭappiṉ aṟivuṭaiyār paṇivar.
taṟpukaḻcci tītu
vilakkiya ōmpi vitittaṉavē ceyyum
nalattakaiyār nalviṉaiyun tītē - pulap pakaiyai
veṉṟaṉam nalloḻukkil niṉṟēm piṟaveṉṟu
tampāṭu tammiṟ koḷiṉ. 18
18. Even the good works of moral men, who shun what is to be avoided, and practise what has been enjoined, become evil, if, proudly boasting that they have conquered the enmity of the senses, and stood stead fastly in a virtuous course, they themselves dwell on their own merits.
(pa-u) pulam pakaiyai piṟa veṉṟaṉam - aimpulaṉkaḷākiya pakaiyaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum veṉṟuviṭṭōm; nal oḻukkil niṉṟēm eṉṟu - nalla oḻukka neṟiyil niṟkiṉṟōmeṉṟu; tam pāṭu tammil koḷiṉ - tammuṭaiya perumaiyait tāmē niṉaittuk koḷvā rāyiṉ; vilakkiya ōmpi - vilakkappaṭṭavaṟṟaic ceyyāmal kāttu; vitittaṉavē ceyyum nalattakaiyār nalviṉaiyum tītē - (ceyyattakkaṉa veṉṟu) vitikkap paṭṭavaikaḷaic ceyyum nalla taṉmaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷ ceyyum nalla ceyalkaḷum tīyaṉavākum.
(ka-u) uyarnta oḻukka neṟiyil niṟpavarkaḷum tammaip pukaḻtal tīyatākum.
taṉṉaip pukaḻāmaiyē naṉṟu
taṉṉai viyappippāṉ taṟpukaḻtal tīccuṭar
naṉṉīr corintu vaḷarttaṟṟāl - taṉṉai
viyavāmai yaṉṟē viyappāva tiṉpam
nayavāmai yaṉṟē nalam. 19
19. To praise oneself in order to attract admiration, is like feeding the flame with pure water - Is not the absence of self-admiration that which is to be admired? Is not happiness freedom from the desire of pleasure?
(pa-u) taṉṉai viyappippāṉ taṉ pukaḻtal - (oruvaṉ) taṉṉaip piṟar matikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutit taṉṉaip pukaḻtal; nal nīr corintu tī cuṭar vaḷarttaṟṟāl - nalla nīrai ūṟṟi neruppuc cuṭarai vaḷarttatu pōlākum; taṉṉai viyavāmai aṉṟē viyappu āvatu - taṉṉaip pukaḻātu iruppataṉṟō nalla matippāvatu; iṉpam nayavāmai aṉṟē nalam - iṉpattai virumpāmal iruppatallavō iṉpamāvatu.
(ka-u) taṉṉaip pukaḻntu pukaḻpeṟa niṉaittal nīr ūṟṟit tī vaḷarttatu pōlākum.
piṟar matippuvēṇṭuvāṉ ceyyat takkavai
piṟarāl peruñcuṭṭu vēṇṭuvāṉ yāṇṭum
maṟavāmē nōṟpatoṉ ṟuṇṭu - piṟarpiṟar
cīrellān tūṟṟic ciṟumai puṟaṅkāttu
yāryārkkum tāḻcci colal 20
20. For him who desires much notice from others, there is one act of penance which he must never forget to practise; continually to dwell upon all the merits, and conceal the defects, of others and to address all with humility.
(pa-u) piṟarāl perum cuṭṭu vēṇṭuvāṉ - piṟarāl matikkappaṭum periya matippai vēṇṭukiṉṟavaṉ; yāṇṭum maṟavāmē nōṟpatu oṉṟu uṇṭu-eppoḻutum maṟavāmal ceyvatu oṉṟu uḷḷatu; (atu) piṟar piṟar cīr ellām tūṟṟi - maṟṟavaratu ciṟappukkaḷai yellām collip parappi; ciṟumai puṟam kāttu - ciṟappallātavaṟṟaip paravāmal aṭakki; yār yārkkum tāḻcci colal - ellāriṭattum vaṇakkamāṉa moḻikaḷaip pēcutal.
(ka-u) piṟar matippai virumpukiṉṟavaṉ maṟṟavar naṉmaikaḷaic collit tīmaikaḷaic collātirukka vēṇṭum. ellāriṭattum vaṇakkamāṉa moḻikaḷaip pēca vēṇṭum.
kaṟṟapaṭi nillātavariṉ coṟcāturiyam
kaṟṟup piṟarkkuraittut tāmnillār vāyppaṭūum
veṟṟuraik kuṇṭōr valiyuṭaimai - coṟṟanīr
nillāta teṉṉeṉṟu nāṇuṟaippa nērttoruvaṉ
collāmē cūḻntu colal 21
21. The empty speech of those, who do not themselves persist in what they have learned, and taught to others has weight only in one respect; in the indirect reproof of those who do not put them to shame by asking directly, why is it that you, who have taught others, do not practise yourself what you teach.
(pa-u) kaṟṟu piṟarkku uraittu tām nillār vāypaṭūum - (nūlkaḷai) paṭittu ak karuttukkaḷaip piṟarkkuc collit tām avvāṟu naṭavātavaruṭaiya vāyiliruntu piṟakkum; veṟu uraikku ōr vali uṭaimai uṇṭu - payaṉillāta collukku oru valimai uḷḷatu; (atu) coṟṟa nīrnillātatu eṉ eṉṟu - (nāṅkaḷ) naṭakka vēṇṭumeṉṟu collukiṉṟa nīr appaṭi naṭavātatu ēṉ eṉṟu; nāṇ uṟaippa nērttu oruvaṉ collāmē cūḻntu colal - nāṇam mika uṇṭākumpaṭi oruvaṉ nērē collātapaṭi ārāyntu colvatākum.
(ka-u) kaṟṟapaṭi tāṉ naṭakkātavar piṟarkkup pōtippatu pētaimai.
poruḷpeṟṟuk kalvi kaṟpittalākātu
piṟarkkup payaṉpaṭat tāṅkaṟṟa viṟpār
tamakkup payaṉvē ṟuṭaiyār - tiṟappaṭūun
tīviṉai yañcā viṟalkoṇṭu teṉpulattār
kōviṉai vēlai koḷal. 22
22. Those, who sell to others the advantages of the mysteries which they have learned, reserve an advantage of another description to themselves; the receiving the labours of the king of the dead, with the hardihood that defies the terrors of an evil life.
(pa-u) piṟarkkup payaṉpaṭa tām kaṟṟa viṟpār - piṟarukkup payaṉākumpaṭi tām kaṟṟavaṟṟaip poruḷukkuk koṭuppavar; tamakku payaṉvēṟu uṭaiyar - tamakku vēṟoru payaṉai uṭaiyavarāvar; (atu) tiṟam paṭūum tīviṉai añcā viṟal koṇṭu teṉpulattār kōviṉai - palavakaippaṭṭa tīya ceyalkaḷaiyum ceyya añcāta vīram koṇṭa teṉpulattār eṉṉum pitirkaḷiṉ aracaṉai; vēlai koḷal - (taṅkaḷai narakattiliṭṭu varuttutalākiya) vēlaiyaic ceyyap paṇṇutal.
(ka-u) poruḷ peṟṟuk koṇṭu kalviyaic collik koṭuttavar narakil varuntuvar.
kaṟṟavar capaiyil kallātār eḻutal
kaṟpaṉa ūḻaṟṟār kalvik kaḻakattāṅ(ku)
oṟkamiṉ ṟūttaivāy aṅkāttal - maṟṟuttam
valluru añcaṉmiṉ eṉpavē māpaṟavai
pulluru añcuva pōl. 23
23. The ceaseless chattering, in the learned assembly, of the coarse lips of those who were not destined to learning, tells us not to fear their otherwise powerful appearance, as the beasts and birds fear a figure of straw.
(pa-u) kaṟpaṉa ūḻ aṟṟār - kaṟkavēṇṭiyavaṟṟaik kaṟkum nalla ūḻ illātavar; kalvi kaḻakattu - kaṟṟavar irukkum capaiyil; oṟkam iṉṟu ūttaivāy aṅkāttal - aṭakkam illāmal ūttai vāyait tiṟantu pēcutal; māpaṟavai puluru añcuva pōl valuru añcaṉ miṉ eṉpavē - vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavaikaḷum pullāṟceytu vaikkappaṭṭa uruvattukku añcuvatu pōlat tammuṭaiya valimaiyāṉa uruvattukku añca vēṇṭām eṉṟu colvatu pōlākum.
(ka-u) kaṟṟavar capaiyil kallātavar eḻuntāl avaraik kaṟṟavar poruṭpaṭuttār.
taṟperumaiyāḷariṭam kalvi cērātu
pōkkaṟu kalvi pulamikkār pālaṉṟi
mīkkoḷ nakaiyiṉār vāyccērā - tākkaṇaṅkum
āṇavām peṇmai yuṭaitteṉiṉum peṇṇalam
pēṭukoḷap paṭuva til. 24
24. Inexhaustible learning is attainable only by those who excel in observation; not by those who smile in vain self-conceit. Though a woman posses charms which Lachmi might envy, they cannot be enjoyed by an Hermaphrodite.
(pa-u) tākku aṇaṅkum āṇ avām peṇmai uṭaittu eṉiṉum - (peṇ) tākki varuttukiṉṟatum āṇkaḷāl virumpappaṭuvatu mākiya peṇmait taṉmai uṭaiyatāyiṉum; peṇ nalam pēṭu koḷap paṭuvatil - peṇṇiṉuṭaiya iṉpam āṇ taṉmai illātavaṉāl koḷḷappaṭuvatillai; (atupōla) pōkku aṟu kalvi - kuṟṟamaṟṟa kalvi; pulam mikkārpāl aṉṟi - aṟivu mikkavariṭattilallāmal; mīk koḷ nakaiyiṉār vāy cērā - taṟperumaiyāl nakaiyāṭukiṉṟa variṭattiṟ cēramāṭṭā.
(ka-u) kalvi taṟperumai koṇṭiruppavarai aṭaiyātu.
mūṭarkku aṟivu koḷutta muṭiyātu
kaṟṟaṉa kallār cevimāṭṭik kaiyuṟūuṅ
kuṟṟan tamatē piṟitaṉṟu - muṟṟuṇarntun
tāmavar taṉmai yuṇarātār tammuṇarā
ētilarai nōva tevaṉ. 25
25. If learned men incur disrespect, while forcing instruction into the ears of the ignorant, the fault is their own. If, with all their knowledge, they do not know their neighbours’ qualities, why should they be hurt at these ignorant neighbours for not knowing their merits.
(pa-u) kaṟṟaṉa - tām kaṟṟavaṟṟai; kallār cevimāṭṭi - mūṭariṉ ceviyil nuḻaikka muyalvatāl; kaiyuṟūum kuṟṟam tamatē - aṭaiyum kuṟṟam avaruṭaiyatē; piṟitu aṉṟu - vēṟoruvaruṭaiyataṉṟu; muṟṟum uṇarntum avar taṉmai uṇarātār - ellām aṟintum ammūṭariṉ taṉmaiyai aṟiyātavar; tam uṇarā ētilarai nōvatu evaṉ - tamatu kuṟṟattai uṇarātu ammūṭarai nontu koḷvatu ēṉ?
(ka-u) mūṭarukkuk kalvi pukaṭṭappukuntu avarkaḷāl avamāṉappaṭṭa kaṟṟavarkaḷ ellām aṟintiruntum ammūṭariṉ taṉmaiyai aṟiyātavarāvar. avarkaḷ tamatu kuṟṟattukku varuntātu ammūṭarai nontukoḷvatu ēṉ?
kīḻōr mēlōr iyalpu
vēttavai kāvār mikaṉmakkaḷ vēṟucilar
kāttatu koṇṭāṅ kuvappeytār - māttakaiya
antap purattatu pūñai puṟaṅkaṭaiya
kantukol pūṭkaik kaḷiṟu. 26
26. The noble wait not in attendance at the royal court: and there are some others, who have no enjoyment in waiting there. The cat frequents the splendid zenana, while the hollow handed elephants, which can tear up the pillars to which they are bound, remain without the gate.
(pa-u) mikal makkaḷ vēttu avai kāvār - uyarntavarkaḷ aracaṉuṭaiya capaiyil (parivāraṅkaḷākak) kāttuk koṇṭirukka māṭṭārkaḷ; vēṟu cilar kāttatu koṇṭu āṅku uvappu eytār - ivarkaḷallāta vēṟu cilar (avvaraca capaiyaik) kāttuk koṇṭu niṉṟum makiḻcciyaṭaiya māṭṭār; mā takaiya antappurattatu pūñai - perumaiyuṭaiya peṇkaḷ taṅkum aṟaiyiṭattup pūṉai irukkum; kantu kol pūṭkai kaḷiṟu puṟaṅkaṭaiyatu - kaṭṭut taṟiyai muṟikkum valiyuṭaiya yāṉai talaivāyilil niṟkum.
vēntu - vēttu eṉa niṉṟatu.
(ka-u) aracaratu parivāraṅkaḷāka iruntum cilar perumai aṭaiyār. avvāṟillāta aṟiñar veḷiyē niṟpiṉum perumai aṭaivar.
ivarkku ivar teyvameṉal
kulamakaṭkut teyvam koḻunaṉē maṉṟa
putalvarkkut tantaiyun tāyum - aṟavōrk
kaṭikaḷē teyvam aṉaivōrkkun teyvam
ilaimukap paimpūṇ iṟai. 27
27. To well-born women, their husband is a god; to children, their father and mother; to the virtuous, their spiritual preceptor is a god; and a god to all, is the kind, adorned with leaf-like ornaments of pale gold.
(pa-u) kulam makaṭku koḻunaṉē teyvam - nalla kuṭiyiṟ piṟanta (oḻukkamuṭaiya) peṇkaḷukkuk kaṇavaṉē teyvam; putalvarkku tantaiyum tāyum (teyvam) - makkaḷukkut tantaiyum tāyum teyvam; aṟavōrkku aṭikaḷē (teyvam) - (nallavaḻiyil cellum) māṇāk karukku āciriyarē teyvam; ellārkkum ilaimukam paimpūṇ iṟai teyvam - ellōrukkum ilaimukam pōṉṟa pacumaiyāṉa poṉ āparaṇaṅkaḷ aṇinta aracaṉ teyvamāvāṉ; maṉṟa - uṟutiyāka.
(ka-u) kulamakaṭkuk kaṇavaṉum, putalvarkkut tantaiyum tāyum, māṇākkarukku āciriyarum, kuṭikaḷ ellōrukkum aracaṉum teyvamāvar.
kuṭikaḷaik kāvāta āṭci payaṉaṟṟatu
kaṇṇiṟ colicceviyiṉ nōkkum iṟaimāṭci
puṇṇiyattiṉ pālatē yāyiṉun - taṇṇaḷiyāṉ
maṉpatai yōmpātārk keṉṉum vayappaṭaimaṟ
ṟeṉpayakkum āṇal lavarkku. 28
28. Though men possess the royal privilege of speaking with the eye, and seeing with the ear, as the fruit of former virtues; of what avail is it, unless they protect the earth with their clemency? What advantage will mighty weapons afford to those, who are wanting in manly courage?
(pa-u) kaṇṇiṉ coli ceviyil nōkkum iṟaimāṭci - kaṇ pārvaiyāl karuttai aṟivittuk kātiṉāl kēṭpatāl kaṇṇāṟ kaṇṭatu pōl aṟiyum aracaṉuṭaiya perumai; puṇṇiyattiṉ pālatē āyiṉum - muṟceyta puṇṇiyattāl uṇṭāvatāṉālum; taṇ aḷiyāl maṉpatai ōmpātārkku - kuḷirnta karuṇaiyāl kuṭikaḷaip pātukāvāta aracarukku; eṉṉum - (avaruṭaiya ciṟappu) eṉṉa payaṉuṭaiyatu? āṇ allavarkku vayappaṭai eṉ payakkum - āṇ taṉmaiyillātavarkku veṟṟi tarattakka āyutam eṉṉa payaṉait tarum?
(ka-u) kuṭikaḷai nalla muṟaiyil kāppāṟṟāviṭṭāl araca atikāram payaṉaṟṟatu.
kuṭikaḷai varutti vari koḷḷalākātu
kuṭikoṉ ṟiṟaikoḷḷuṅ kōmakaṟkuk kaṟṟā
maṭikoṉṟu pālkoḷalum māṇpē - kuṭiyōmpik
koḷḷumā koḷvōrkkuk kāṇṭumē mānitiyam
veḷḷattiṉ mēlum pala. 29
29. To a prince, who destroys his subjects to obtain his revenue, to cut open the udder of a new milched cow, to obtain her milk, would be comparatively creditable; but to those who protect their subjects before receiving their own dues, we have seen wealth overflowing like a flood.
(pa-u) kuṭi koṉṟu iṟai koḷḷum kōmakaṟku - kuṭikaḷai varutti varivāṅkum aracaṉukku; kaṉṟu ā maṭikoṉṟu pāl koḷalum māṇpē - kaṉṟuṭaiya pacuviṉ maṭiyai aṟuttup pāl eṭuppatum nalla ceyalākum; kuṭi ōmpi koḷḷumā koḷvōrkku - kuṭikaḷaip pātukāttu vāṅkum muṟaimaiyil (variyai) vāṅkum aracarukku; mānitiyam veḷḷattiṉ mēlum pala kāṇṭum -periya celvam veḷḷattiṟkum atikamākap perukuvataik kāṇkiṉṟōm.
(ka-u) kuṭikaḷai varuttātu varikoḷḷum aracaṉiṉ
celvam vaḷarum.
koṭuṅkōl aracaṉ iyalpu
iṉṟu koḷaṟpāla nāḷaik koḷappoṟāṉ
niṉṟu kuṟaiyirappa nērpaṭāṉ - ceṉṟoruvaṉ
āvaṉa kūṟiṉ eyiṟalaippāṉ āṟalaikkum
vēṭalaṉ vēntum alaṉ. 30
30. He, who will not wait till to-morrow, to receive, that which becomes due to-day; who will not attend, when one stands to beg redress; and who will gnash his teeth with impatience when one may approach to offer wise advice; is neither a Vedan who robs on the highway, nor a king.
(pa-u) iṉṟu koḷaṟpāla nāḷai koḷap poṟāṉ - iṉṟu vāṅkavēṇṭiya poruḷai nāḷaikku vāṅkap poṟuttirukka māṭṭāṉ; niṉṟu kuṟai irappa nērpaṭāṉ - (kuṭikaḷ) muṉṉē niṉṟu tamatu kuṟaikaḷaic colla akappaṭamāṭṭāṉ; oruvaṉ ceṉṟu āvaṉa kūṟa eyiṟu alaippāṉ - oruvaṉ ceṉṟu taṉakku ākavēṇṭiyavaṟṟaic coṉṉāl pallaik kaṭittuk kōpippāṉ; āṟu alaikkum vēṭu alaṉ vēntum alaṉ - (avaṉ) vaḻippaṟi ceyyum vēṭaṉumallaṉ aracaṉum allaṉ.
(ka-u) kuṭikaḷai varuttip poruḷ koḷḷum aracaṉ vaḻipaṟikkum vēṭaṉilum koṭiyavaṉ.
araca celvattil mayakkam
muṭippa muṭittuppiṉ pūcuva pūci
uṭuppa uṭuttuṇpa uṇṇā - iṭittiṭittuk
kaṭṭurai kūṟiṟ cevikoḷā kaṇviḻiyā
neṭṭuyirppō ṭuṟṟa piṇam. 31
31. A king, who after crowning his head with chaplets, rubbing his body with unguents, clothing himself in rich robes, and feasting on rich viands, will not lend his ear, nor open his eyes, though addressed with reiterated earnestness, is but a corpse endued with breath.
(pa-u) muṭippa muṭittu - muṭiyiṟ cūṭattakka (pūmālaikaḷaic) cūṭikkoṇṭu; piṉ pūcuva pūci - piṉpu uṭaliṟ pūcavēṇṭiya (puṉuku cantaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟaip) pūcik koṇṭu; uṭuppa uṭuttu - uṭukka vēṇṭiya (uṭaikaḷai) uṭuttuk koṇṭu; uṇpa uṇṇā - uṇṇattakka (nalla uṇavukaḷai) uṇṭu koṇṭu, iṭittu iṭittu kaṭṭurai kūṟiṉ - palamuṟai nerukki uṟuti moḻikaḷaic coṉṉālum; cevi kēḷā - kātiṉāṟ kēḷāmalum; kaṇ viḻiyā - kaṇkaḷāṟ pārāmalum (irukkiṉṟa aracar); neṭṭuyirppōṭu uṟṟa piṇam - mūccu viṭukiṉṟa piṇaṅkaḷāvar.
muṭittup pūci uṭuttu uṇṭu kēḷātu pārātu irukkum aracar mūccuviṭum piṇattukkuc camam.
(ka-u) araca celvattil mayaṅkik kuṭikaḷiṉ kuṟaikaḷaik kēḷāta aracaṉ piṇattukkuc camam.
aracaṉ ceyyavēṇṭiyavai
ōṟṟiṟ ṟeriyā ciṟaippuṟat tōrtumeṉap
poṟṟōḷ tuṇaiyāt teritantum - kuṟṟam
aṟivariteṉ ṟañcuvatē ceṅkōṉmai ceṉṟu
kuṟaiyiṭiṉuṅ kēḷāmai yaṉṟu. 32
32. The duty of him who holds a sceptre. is to fear on account of the difficulty of detecting wrong; even though, assured that it cannot be ascertained by spies, he has in person made private inquiry, his own arm adorned with gold being his only protection; not to refuse to listen, though men come and complain of it.
(pa-u) oṟṟil teriyā - oṟṟarāl aṟiya muṭiyātavaikaḷai; ciṟaippuṟattu ōrtum eṉa - otukkiṭaṅkaḷil niṉṟu aṟivōmeṉṟu; poṉ tōḷ tuṇaiyā teritantum - poṉvaḷai aṇinta (taṉatu) tōḷē taṉakkut tuṇaiyāka aṅku niṉṟu aṟintum; kuṟṟam aṟivu aritu eṉṟu añcuvatē ceṅkōṉmai - kuṟṟam yātu eṉṟu aṟiya muṭiyavillaiyē eṉṟu aracaṉ payappaṭuvatē nīti āṭciyākum; ceṉṟu kuṟaiyiṭiṉum kēḷāmai aṉṟu - ceṉṟu kuṟaikaḷai muṟaiyiṭṭālum kēḷāmal iruppataṉṟu.
(ka-u) aracaṉuṭaiya ciṟappiyalpu kuṭikaḷiṉ kuṟaikaḷai aṟintu avaikaḷait tīrttalākum.
poy nīkki mey koḷḷa vēṇṭum
ētilār yātum pukala iṟaimakaṉ
kōtorīik koḷkai mutukkuṟaivu - nērniṉṟu
kākkai veḷiteṉpār eṉcollār tāykkolai
cālpuṭait teṉpāru muṇṭu. 33
33. While others assert every thing, the wisdom of the Prince is to receive their assertions, rejecting what is false. What will not men say, who standing opposite to a crow, will call it white? There are some, who will even say, that the murder of a mother is noble.
(pa-u) ētilār yātum pukala - piṟar tamakkut tōṉṟiyavāṟu ellām colla; iṟaimakaṉ kōtuorīi koḷkai - aracaṉ avarkaḷ kūṟiya vaṟṟiṉ kuṟṟamāṉavai nīkki nallavaṟṟaik koḷḷutal; mutukkuṟaivu - aṟivuṭaimaiyākum; nēr niṉṟu kākkai veḷiteṉpār eṉ colār - kākattukku muṉṉāl niṉṟu atu veṇṇiṟamuṭaiya teṉṟu collu kiṉṟavarkaḷ etaic colla māṭṭārkaḷ; tāykkolai cālpu uṭaittu eṉpārum uṇṭu - tāyaik kolai ceytal nīti eṉṟu kūṟu kiṉṟavarkaḷum ivvulakattil uṇṭu.
(ka-u) piṟar kūṟukiṉṟavaṟṟiṟ poyn nīkki uṇmai koḷḷutal aracarkku aṟivuṭaimai.
mūṭar kēṭaṟiyār
kaṇkūṭāp paṭṭatu kēṭeṉiṉuṅ kīḻmakkaṭ(ku)
uṇṭō vuṇarccimaṟ ṟillākum - maṇṭeri
tāṉvāy maṭuppiṉum mācuṇam kaṇṭuyilva
pērā perumūc ceṟintu 34
34. Though ruin be before their eyes, will fools perceive it? No, they will not: though thick flames envelope their body, the sleeping rock serpents will not arouse from their loud-breathing slumbers.
(pa-u) kēṭu kaṇkūṭu ā paṭṭatu eṉiṉum - (tamakku) varuṅkēṭu kaṇṇukkut teripavāyiṉum; kīḻmakkaṭku uṇṭō uṇarcci - mūṭarukku ataṉiṉṟu tappippiḻaikkum uṇarcci uḷḷatō? illākum - illai yākum; maṇṭu eritāṉ vāy maṭuppiṉum - mikka nerupput taṉṉaic cūḻn tālum; mācuṇam perumūcceṟintu kaṇ tuyilva - pāmpu perumūccu viṭṭu nittirai koḷḷum; pērā - avviṭattaiviṭṭu nīṅkātu.
(ka-u) āpattu aṇmaiyil iruppatai aṟintālum kīḻmakkaḷ ataṉai uṇarār.
kēṭṭukku aṟikuṟi
naṭpup pirittal pakainaṭṭal oṟṟikaḻtal
pakkattār yāraiyum aiyuṟutal takkār
neṭumoḻi kōṟal kuṇampiṟi tātal
keṭuvatu kāṭṭuṅ kuṟi. 35
35. The estrangement of friends, the receiving of enemies into friendship; to scoff at a spy, and to suspect all associates; to despise the continued advice of the wise; and become changed in disposition, are signs of approaching ruin.
(pa-u) naṭpup pirittal - naṇparkaḷaip pirintu pōkumpaṭi ceytalum; pakai naṭṭal - pakaivarai naṇparākkik koḷḷutalum; oṟṟu ikaḻtal - oṟṟarai avamatittalum; pakkattār yāraiyum aiyuṟutal - kūṭa iruppārkaḷ takkār neṭumo kōṟal - aṟivuṭaiyavariṉ ellārai yum cantēkittalum; aṟivuraikaḷai aḻittalum; kuṇam piṟitu ātal - nallakuṇattiliruntu māṟupaṭutal; keṭuvatu kāṭṭum kuṟi - (ākiya ivaikaḷ) varappōkum kēṭṭaik kāṭṭum aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷākum.
(ka-u) naṭpup pirittal mutaliyaṉa kēṭu varuvataṟ kaṟikuṟikaḷākum.
celvac cerukkāl periyāraip piḻaittal
paṇiyap paṭuvār puṟaṅkaṭaiya rākat
taṇivil kaḷippiṉāl tāḻvārk - kaṇiya(tu)
iḷaiyāḷ muyakkeṉiṉuñ cēyttaṉṟē mūttāḷ
tolaiyāta 1pōkaṅ koḷal. 36
36. The men, who are sunk in immoderate delights, while those who ought to be honoured are waiting at the gate, though now they embrace the goddess of prosperity, are not far from the enjoyment of the charms of the close bosom of her elder sister, adversity.
(pa-u) paṇiyappaṭuvār puṟaṅkaṭaiyar āka - tām vaṇaṅki etirkoḷḷattakkavar talaivāyilil niṟka; taṇivu il kaḷippiṉāl tāḻvārkku - aṭaṅkāta celvac cerukkiṉāl (avarai etir koḷḷa) kālam nīṭṭippavarukku; iḷaiyāḷ muyakku aṇiyatu eṉiṉum - ilakkumiyiṉ cērkkai avarkkuk kiṭṭa irunta pōtum; mūttāḷ tolaiyāta pōkam koḷal cēyttu aṉṟu - mūtēviyiṉ nīṅkāta cērkkaiyaip peṟutal tolaivil illai.
(ka-u) celvac cerukkiṉāl periyārai matiyāviṭṭāl mūtēvi kuṭi koḷvaḷ.
naṟkuṇamuṭaiyōr paṇpu
kaṇṇōk karumpā nakaimukamē nāṇmalarā
iṉmoḻiyiṉ vāymaiyē tīṅkāyā - vaṇmai
palamā nalaṅkaṉinta paṇpuṭaiyā raṉṟē
caliyāta kaṟpa taru. 37
37. The nobly generous whose glance is a bud; their smiling countenance, a fresh blown blossom; the truth of their courteous speech, the sweet young fruit; and munificence, the ripe fruit; are not these the real inexhaustible calpa tree?
(pa-u) kaṇ nōkku arumpu ā - kaṇṇāl pārkkum pārvai arumpākavum; nakai mukamē nāḷ malar ā - malarnta mukamē putup pūvākavum; iṉmoḻiyiṉ vāymaiyē tīm kāy ā - iṉiya colliṉ uṇmaiyē iṉippāṉa kāyākavum; vaṇmai palam ā - koṭaiyē paḻam ākavum; nalam kaṉinta paṇpuṭaiyār aṉṟē caliyāta kaṟpaka taru - naṉmaikaḷ paḻutta naṟkuṇamuṭaiyavar allavō iḷaittal illāta kaṟpaka maramāvar.
(ka-u) niṟainta kuṇamuṭaiyavar collālum ceyalkaḷālum iṉiyarāyiruppar.
yāṉaiyuṇaviṉ oruciṟitu kōṭi eṟumpiṟku uṇavu
vāṅkuṅ kavaḷat toruciṟitu vāytappiṉ
tūṅkuṅ kaḷiṟō tuyaruṟā - āṅkatukoṇ(ṭu)
ūrum eṟumpiṅ korukōṭi yuyyumāl
āruṅ kiḷaiyō ṭayiṉṟu. 38
38. The drowsy elephant will not grudge the few grains which may fall from the ball of rice that it is receiving: the creeping ants will pick them up, and ten million living creatures will subsist, feasting with their crowded progeny.
(pa-u) vāṅkum kavaḷattu oru ciṟitu vāy tappiṉ - tāṉ vāṅki uṇkiṉṟa oruvāy uṇaviṉ oru ciṟu pakuti vāykkuḷ pōkātu tavaṟi viḻuntuviṭṭāl; tūṅkum kaḷiṟu ō tuyar uṟātu - acaintu koṇṭu niṟkum yāṉaiyō tuṉpam aṭaiyamāṭṭātu; āṅku atu koṇṭu - avviṭattil viḻunta ataṉaik koṇṭu; iṅku ūrum eṟumpu oru kōṭi ārum kiḷaiyōṭu ayiṉṟu uyyum - ivvulakil ūrukiṉṟa eṟumpukaḷil oru kōṭi taṅkaḷ niṟainta cuṟṟattōṭu uṇavai uṇṭu uyir vāḻum.
(ka-u) yāṉaikkuk koṭukkum kavaḷa uṇaviṉ oru ciṟitu kōṭi eṟumpukku uṇavākum.
aracaṉuṭaiya naṉ matippu
mākañ ciṟukak kuvittu nitikkuvai
īkaiyiṉ ēkkaḻuttam mikkuṭaiya - mākol
pakaimukatta veḷvēlāṉ pārvaiyiṟ ṟīṭṭum
nakaimukatta naṉku matippu. 39
39. The smiling approbation, which is expressed in the glance of the prince, whose bright javelin threatens the death of wild beasts; is a prouder thing, than the gift of a heap of treasure, large enough to diminish the sky.
(pa-u) pakai mukatta mā kol veḷ vēlāṉ - pakaivaratu pōrkkaḷattē kutiraiyaik kolkiṉṟa veḷḷiya vēlaiyuṭaiya aracaṉ; nakai mukatta pārvaiyil tīṭṭum naṉku matippu - malarnta mukattiluḷḷa kaṇkaḷāl tōṟṟuvikkum naṉmatippukaḷ; mākam ciṟuka kuvitta nitikkuvai īkaiyiṉ - mēkamum ciṟitu (eṉṉumpaṭi) kuvitta poruḷ kuviyalaik koṭuttaliṉum pārkka; ēkkaḻuttam mikkuṭaiya- perumitam periyatākum.
(ka-u) aracaṉ naṉmatippaip peṟutal poṉ kuviyalaip peṟutalilum perumai taruvatu.
aṭaikkalam kāttal
kaḷaikaṇāt tammaṭaintārk kuṟṟuḻiyum maṟṟōr
viḷaivuṉṉi veṟṟuṭampu tāṅkār - taḷarnaṭaiya(tu)
ūṉuṭam peṉṟu pukaḻuṭam pōmputaṟkē
tāṉuṭam paṭṭārka ṭām. 40
40. Those will not spare their worthless bodies, in the hope of any future profit, should anything happen even to such as are under their protection; who, sensible how frail is the body of flesh, have determined to preserve the body of glory.
(pa-u) ūṉ uṭampu taḷar naṭaiyatu eṉṟu - iṟaicciyālāṉa ivvuṭampu nilaitaḷarum iyalpuṭaiyatu eṉṟu karuti; pukaḻ uṭampu ōmputaṟku uṭampaṭṭārkaḷ tām - pukaḻ uṭampai vaḷarppataṟkut tīrmāṉittavarkaḷ; tam kaḷaikaṇ ā aṭaintārkku-tamatu ātaravaināṭi vantu cērntavarkku; uṟṟu uḻiyum - (oru ciṟiya tuṉpam) vantaviṭattum; maṟṟu ōr viḷaivu uṉṉi veṟṟi uṭampu tāṅkār - (pukaḻallāta) vēṟoṉṟaṉai virumpit tamatu ūṉuṭampai vaittirukkamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) ulakil tampukaḻai nāṭṭa virumpuvōr tamatu uṭalaik koṭuttum tammai aṭaikkalamāka vantaṭaintavaraik kāppar.
matippai iḻattalākātu
tammuṭai yāṟṟalum māṉamun tōṟṟuttam
iṉṉuyir ōmpiṉum ōmpuka - piṉṉarc
ciṟuvarai yāyiṉum maṉṟa tamakkāṅ(ku)
iṟuvarai yillai yeṉiṉ. 41
41. If any would save their sweet lives, by sacrificing courage and honour; let them to do so, provided even then they can be secure from death, for ever so short a time.
(pa-u) piṉṉar ciṟuvarai āyiṉum maṉṟa tamakku iṟuvarai illai eṉiṉ - iṉimēl ciṟitu kāla aḷavāyiṉum tamakkut tiṇṇamākac cāvu illaiyāṉāl; tammuṭai āṟṟalum māṉamum tōṟṟu - tamatu valimaiyaiyum matippaiyum iḻantu; tam iṉṉuyir ōmpiṉum ōmpuka - tamatu iṉiya uyiraik kāppāṟṟiṉum kāppāṟṟuka. ‘āṅku’ iṭaiccol.
(ka-u) iṉimēl maraṇamillaiyeṉṟu kaṇṭāl taṉatu māṉattaiyum matippaiyum oruvaṉ iḻantāyiṉum taṉ uyiraik kāppāṟṟalām.
cāvukku añcātavar
kalaṉaḻinta kaṟpuṭaip peṇṭirum aintu
pulaṉoṭuṅkap poyyoḻin tārum - kolaiñāṭpiṉ
moympuṭai vīrarum añcār muraṇmaṟali
tumpai muṭicū ṭiṉum. 42
42. Virtuous matrons, who have laid aside their ornaments in widowhood; those, who, having done away with falsehood, have brought the five senses to agree: and warriors, who, in the slaughterous field of battle, retain their courage; fear not, though grim Yaman assume the chaplet of Drona flowers.
(pa-u) kalaṉ aḻinta kaṟpuṭai peṇṭirum - kaṇavaṉ iṟattalāl nakaikaḷaik kaḷainta kaṟpuṭaiya peṇkaḷum; aintu pulaṉ oṭuṅka poy oḻintārum - (mey vāy kaṇ mūkkuc cevi eṉṉum) aintu pulaṉkaḷum kuṉṟumpaṭi poyyai viṭṭavarum; kolai ñāṭpiṉ moympu uṭai vīrarum - pōrkkaḷattiluḷḷa valiya vīrarum; muraṇ maṟali tumpai cūṭiṉum añcār - valiya iyamaṉ taṉatu muṭiyilē tumpai mālai cūṭiṉālum (etirttālum) añcamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) kaṇavaṉai iḻanta kaṟpuṭai makaḷirum, tuṟavikaḷum, vīrarum uyiraiviṭa añcār.
meyyaṟivuṭaiyōr cāka añcār
puḻuneḷintu puṇṇaḻuki yōcaṉai nāṟum
kaḻimuṭai nāṟṟatta vēṉum - viḻalar
viḷivuṉṉi veytuyirppar meyppayaṉ koṇṭār
cuḷiyār cumaipōṭu taṟku 43
43. Fools will sigh deeply at the thought of death, though their bodies be corrupted with sores full of crawling worms, the rank odour of which may be perceived at the distance of five miles; but those, who have derived the true advantage of the body, will not shudder to lay down the burden.
(pa-u) puṇ aḻuki puḻu neḷintu yōcaṉai nāṟum kaḻimuṭai nāṟṟatta ēṉum - (tamatu uṭal) puṇṇāṉatu aḻukip puḻu neḷintu oru yōcaṉai tūram mikutiyāka vīcukiṉṟa tīya nāṟṟam uṭaiyatāyirunta pōtilum; viḻalar - aṟivillātavar; viḷivu uṉṉi veytu uyirppar - iṟattalai añci perumūccu viṭuvar; mey payaṉ koṇṭār - uṭam piṉālākiya payaṉai aṭainta aṟivuṭaiyōr; cumai pōṭutaṟku cuḷiyār - uṭampākiya cumaiyai viṭutaṟku naṭuṅkamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) uṭampiṉ payaṉākiya meyyaṟivaip peṟṟōr cākap payappaṭār.
aracaṉ evvaḻi kuṭikaḷu mavvaḻi
ikaḻi ṉikaḻntāṅ kiṟaimakaṉ oṉṟu
pukaḻiṉum okkap pukaḻpa - ikaṉmaṉṉaṉ
cīrvaḻip paṭṭatē maṉpataimaṟ ṟeṉceyum
nīrvaḻip paṭṭa puṇai. 44
44. If the prince speak contemptuously, they will speak so likewise; and if he praise, they will praise in equal terms, thus the world are led according to the taste of the warrior king. What else does the boat, that is borne along the current of the water?
(pa-u) iṟaimakaṉ oṉṟu ikaḻiṉ - aracaṉ oṉṟai ikaḻntāl; (okka) ikaḻntu - (kuṭikaḷum) tāmumkūṭa atai ikaḻntu; pukaḻiṉum okka pukaḻpa - (aracaṉ) pukaḻntāl tāmum kūṭap pukaḻvar; puṇai nīr vaḻippaṭṭa - teppam nīr cellum vaḻi cellum; maṉpatai ikal maṉṉaṉ cīr vaḻippaṭṭatē - (atupōla) makkaṭ kūṭṭamum valiya aracaṉiṉ ciṟanta vaḻiyilēyē cellum; maṟṟu eṉ ceyum - (avvāṟu ceyvatait tavira) vēṟu eṉṉa ceyya muṭiyum?
(ka-u) aracaṉ āṇaippaṭiyē kuṭikaḷum naṭakka nēriṭum.
nal amaiccar iyalpu
cevicuṭac ceṉṟāṅ kiṭittaṟivu mūṭṭi
vekuḷiṉum vāyverīip pērā - kaviḻmatatta
kaimmā vayattatō pākumaṟ ṟettiṟattum
ammāṇ piṉavē amaiccu. 45
45. The keeper approaches the elephants when liquid flows from its temples in the season of frantic violence; and, as he perfects its discipline by repeated blows until its ear burns with pain, however enraged it be, flinches not in fear, but keeps the charge: such is every respect is the duty of the King’s minister.
(pa-u) pāku - (yāṉaiyaic celuttum) pākaṉ; ettiṟattum kaviḻ matatta kaimā vayattatō - evvaḷavum matam coriyum yāṉaiyai aṭakkātu ataṟku aṭaṅkuvāṉō? aṭaṅkāṉ; (atu pōl) amaiccu - mantiri; (ettiṟattum) ammāṇ piṉavē - evvakaiyilum at taṉmaiyarē āvar; ceṉṟu - (aracaṉ neṟi tavaṟiyaviṭattu avaṉiṭam) pōy; cevi cuṭa iṭittu aṟivu mūṭṭi - (avaṉatu) cevikaḷ varuntum paṭi iṭitturaittu aṟivu uṇṭākac ceytu; vekuḷiṉum vāy verīi pērā - kōpittālum (avaṉukkup) payantu nīṅkār. ‘āṅku’ acai.
(ka-u) aracaṉ kōpittālum avaṉai viṭṭu nīṅkātu amaiccar avaṉukku nallaṟivu pukaṭṭuvar.
aracaṉiṭam camayamaṟintu pēcavēṇṭum
kaivarum vēntaṉ namakkeṉṟu kātalitta
cevvi teriyā turaiyaṟka - ovvorukāl
eṇmaiya ṉēṉum ariyaṉ peritammā
kaṇṇilaṉ uḷveyarppiṉāṉ. 46
46. Utter not all you desire, without observing the proper season, in the belief that the king is under your influence; though occasionally he may be easy to manage; yet how difficult is he when destitute of mercy, and burning with inward rage?
(pa-u) vēntaṉ namakku kaivarum eṉṟu - aracaṉ namakku iṇaṅki varuvāṉ eṉṟu niṉaittu; cevvi teriyātu kātalitta uraiyaṟka - camayam teriyāmal (tām) virumpiyataic collātiruttal vēṇṭum; ovvorukāl eṇmaiyaṉ ēṉum - ciṟcila camayaṅkaḷil (kēṭṭa vaṟṟaip peṟutaṟku) eḷiyaṉpōl tōṉṟiṉālum; peritu ariyaṉ kaṇ ilaṉ uḷ veyarppiṉāṉ - avaṉ (oṉṟaik koṭuttaṟkum kāṇṭaṟkum) mika ariyaṉ irakkamillātavaṉ uḷkōpa muḷḷavaṉ.
(ka-u) aracaṉiṭam camayamaṟintu pēci virumpiyavaṟṟaik kēṭṭup peṟutal vēṇṭum.
aracar iyalpu
paḻamai kaṭaippiṭiyār kēṇmaiyum pārār
kiḻamai piṟitoṉṟuṅ koḷḷār - vekuḷiṉmaṉ
kātaṉmai yuṇṭō iṟaimāṇṭārk kētilarum
ārvalarum illai avarkku. 47
47. They remember not old acquaintance; they regard not intimacy, they acknowledge not the ties of blood, or other connexion; what affection have princes in their rage? They know neither foes nor friends.
(pa-u) vekuḷiṉ maṉ - (aracar) mikak kōpittāl; paḻamai kaṭaippiṭiyār - paḻaiya paḻakkattaip paṟṟi niṉaiyār; kēṇmaiyum pārār - naṇpar eṉpataiyum nōkkār; piṟitu - ivaiyallāmal: kiḻamai oṉṟum koḷḷār - ivar namakku uriyavar eṉpataic ciṟitum niṉaiyār; iṟai māṇṭārkku kātaṉmai uṇṭō - araciyalil āḻntavarkku aṉpu eṉa oṉṟu uṇṭō; avarkku ētilarum ārvalarum ilar - avarkaḷukku ayalāreṉavum cuṟṟattāreṉavum kiṭaiyātu.
(ka-u) aracar kōpittāl ayalavar cuṟṟattavareṉṟum pārār.
aracar talaivāyil kāttal
maṉṉar puṟaṅkaṭai kāttu vaṟitēyām
ennalaṅ kāṇṭumeṉ ṟeḷḷaṟka - paṉṉeṭunāḷ
kāttavai yellāṅ kaṭaimuṭaipōyk kaikoṭuttu
vēttavaiyiṉ mikkuc ceyum. 48
48. Say not contemptuously, “What good have we seen in our fruitless attendance at the king’s gate?” The many long days that you have waited, will at last, in turn afford a hand, to raise you to dignity in the Royal Court.
(pa-u) yām maṉṉar puṟaṅkaṭai vaṟitē kāttum - nām aracaruṭaiya talaivāyilai vīṇē kāttu vantōm; e nalam kāṇṭum eṉṟu eḷḷaṟka - evvakaiyāṉa palaṉaiyum peṟṟilēm eṉṟu ikaḻntu (kavalaip paṭā)tiruttal vēṇṭum; palneṭunāḷ kāttavai ellām - mika nīṇṭa kālam kāttavai ellām; kaṭaimuṟai pōy kai koṭuttu - muṭiviṟ ceṉṟu utaviyāki; vēntu avaiyiṉ mikku ceyum - araca capaiyil uyarvaik koṭukkum.
(ka-u) araca ūḻiyattāl muṭivil uyarvu uṇṭākum.
nalla karumattai muyaṉṟu ceyyavēṇṭum
uṟuti payappa kaṭaipōkā vēṉum
iṟuvarai kāṟum muyalpa - iṟumuyirkku
āyuḷ maruntoḻukkal tītaṉṟāl allaṉapōl
āvaṉavum uṇṭu cila. 49
49. Although you may not be certain of the event, persevere to the last in a good undertaking. It is not wrong to drop the medicine of life into the lips of dying men; for there are some things, which are possible, though they are like things that are not.
(pa-u) uṟuti payappa kaṭaipōkā ēṉum - nalla kāriyaṅkaḷ muṟṟup peṟutal niccayamalla vāyiṉum; iṟuvarai kāṟum muyalpa - (aṟivuṭai yōr) avai muṭivu aṭaiyum varaiyum muyaṟci ceyvar; iṟum uyirkku āyuḷ maruntu oḻukkal tītu aṉṟu - cākum taruṇattiluḷḷa uyirukku uyirai nīṭikkat takka maruntai vāyil viṭutal tītalla; allaṉapōl āvaṉavum cila uṇṭu - tīmaiyākavum cila muṭivatu uṇṭu.
(ka-u) muṭiyāteṉat tōṉṟiṉālum nalla karumattaik kaṭaicivaraiyum muyaṉṟu pārkka vēṇṭum.
ūḻai nampiyirātu muyalavēṇṭum
muyalātu vaittu muyaṟṟiṉmai yālē
uyalākā ūḻttiṟatta veṉṉār - mayalāyum
ūṟṟamil tūviḷakkam ūḻuṇmai kāṇṭumeṉ
tēṟṟār eṟikāl mukattu 50
50. The wise will not say, “Though exertion be laid aside, the decrees of fate cannot, on that account, fail”. However ignorant, none will set up a clear light, without any protection in a strong wind, in order to see the reality of destiny.
(pa-u) muyalātu vaittu - tām muyaṟci ceyyāmal iruntu; ūḻ tiṟatta - ellām ūḻviṉaiyāl uṇṭākum eṉṟu; muyaṟṟu iṉmai yāl uyal ākā eṉṟār - muyaṟci ceyyāmal iruppatāl avai tavaṟippōka māṭṭā eṉṟu (aṟivuṭaiyōr) niṉaittuk koṇṭirār; ūṟṟam il tū viḷakkam - acaivillātu eriyakkūṭiya nalla viḷakkai; ūḻ uṇmai kāṇṭum eṉṟu - ūḻviṉaiirukkum taṉmaiyaik kāṇpōmeṉṟu; eṟikāl mukattu mayalāyum ēṟṟār - vīcukiṉṟa kāṟṟukku etirē aṟivu keṭṭum (evarum) koḷuttamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) ellām ūḻāl varum eṉṟu nampiyirātu muyaṟci ceyya vēṇṭum.
ūḻai muyaṟciyāl vellalām
ulaiyā muyaṟci kaḷaikaṇā vūḻiṉ
valicintum vaṉmaiyu muṇṭē - ulakaṟiyap
pāṉmuḷai tiṉṟu maṟali uyirkuṭitta
kāṉmuḷaiyē cāluṅ kari. 51
51. There is a power, which, with the aid of unyielding exertion, will destroy the strength of destiny. Of this, as is known to all the world, the child, who devoured the germ of fate, and drank the life of Yaman, is an evidence.
(pa-u) ulaiyā muyaṟci kaḷaikaṇ āka - acaivillāta muyaṟciyē tuṇaiyāka; ūḻiṉ vali cintum vaṉmaiyum uṇṭē (tīya) ūḻiṉ valiyaik keṭukkum vaṉmaiyum uḷḷatākum; pāl muḷai tiṉṟu maṟali uyir kuṭitta kāṉmuḷaiyē cālumkari - ūḻviṉaiyiṉ muḷaiyait tiṉṟu iyamaṉiṉ uyirai uṇṭa kuḻantaiyē (mārkkaṇṭēyarē) pōtumāṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭām.
(ka-u) muyaṟciyāl ūḻaiyum vellalām.
āyvaṉa āyntu ceyka
kāla maṟintāṅ kiṭamaṟintu ceyviṉaiyiṉ
mūla maṟintu viḷaivaṟintu - mēluntām
cūḻvaṉa cūḻntu tuṇaimai valiterintu
āḷviṉai yāḷap paṭum. 52
52. With a knowledge of the proper time and place, and of the root and fruit of the undertaking; after due deliberation; and with a knowledge of the strength of existing resources; an enterprize may be accomplished.
(pa-u) kālam aṟintu - (oru karumattaic ceytaṟkēṟṟa) kālattai aṟintu; āṅku iṭam aṟintu - appoḻutē (ceytaṟkēṟṟa) iṭattai yum aṟintu; ceyviṉaiyiṉ mūlam aṟintu - ceyyun toḻiliṉ palattaiyum (āḻattai) aṟintu; viḷaivu aṟintu - peṟum pēṟṟaiyum aṟintu; mēlumtām cūḻvaṉa cūḻntu - mēlum tām ārāya vēṇṭi yavaikaḷai ārāyntu; tuṇaimai vali terintu - tamakkut tuṇai niṟpavariṉ valimaiyaiyum aṟintu; āḷviṉai āḷappaṭum - piṉṉar tamatu muyaṟciyaic ceytal vēṇṭum.
(ka-u) kālam iṭam mutaliyavaikaḷai ārāyntu karumattait toṭaṅkavēṇṭum.
karumamē kaṇṇāyiṉār
meyvaruttam pārār pacinōkkār kaṇtuñcār
evvevar tīmaiyum mēṟkoḷḷār - cevvi
arumaiyum pārarar avamatippuṅ koḷḷār
karumamē kaṇṇā yiṉār. 53
53. They regard not bodily pain; they consider not hunger; they indulge not their eyes in sleep; they esteem not the injury of any; they look not to the rarity of a seasonable opportunity; they care not for contempt; whose eye is fixed on their undertaking.
(pa-u) karumamē kaṇṇāyiṉār - (tamatu) kāriyattai muṭippatilē karuttāyiruppavar; mey varuttam pārār - uṭal varuttattaip poruṭpaṭuttār; paci nōkkār - paciyaik kavaṉikka māṭṭār; kaṇ tuñcār - kaṇ uṟaṅkamāṭṭār; evar evar tīmaiyum mēṟkoḷḷār - yār yār eṉṉa tīmaiyaic ceytālum poruṭpāṭutta māṭṭār; cevvi arumaiyum pārār - kālattiṉ arumaiyaiyum pārkka māṭṭār; avamatippum koḷḷār - piṟariṉ avamatippaiyum karutamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) kāriyattai muṭikkum karuttuṭaiyavar etaiyum poruṭpaṭuttār.
pakai ciṟitāyiṉum kēṭu viḷaiyum
ciṟiya pakaiyeṉiṉum ōmputal tēṟṟār
peritum piḻaipā ṭuṭaiyar - niṟaikayat
tāḻnīr maṭuviṟ ṟavaḷai kutippiṉum
yāṉai niḻalkāṇ paritu. 54
