54. Those, who perceive not, that they ought to beware of even the weakest foe, are greatly in error; if a frog only leap into the deep water of the low pool, it is impossible to see the shadow of an elephant.
(pa-u) pakai ciṟiya eṉiṉum - (tamakkuḷḷa) pakaikaḷ ciṟiyaṉavā yiṉum; ōmputal tēṟṟār piḻaipāṭuṭaiyar - (tammaip) pātukāttuk koḷḷa aṟiyātavar piḻaipāṭuṭaiyavarāvar; nīrniṟai āḻ kayattu maṭuvil - nīr niṟainta āḻnta nīrnilaiyākiya kuḷattil; tavaḷai kutip piṉum yāṉai niḻal kāṇpu aritu - tavaḷai kutittālum (nīr kalaṅkip pōvatāl) yāṉaiyiṉ (periya) niḻaltāṉum teriyātu maṟaintupōkum.
(ka-u) pakai ciṟitāyiṉum kēṭu uṇṭākum.
uṭpakai añcappaṭum
puṟappakai kōṭiyiṉ mikkuṟiṉum añcār
akappakai oṉṟañcik kāppa - aṉaittulakum
colloṉṟiṉ yāppār parintōmpik kāppavē
palkāluṅ kāmap pakai. 55
55. Those, who fear not the attack of more than ten million avowed enemies, should beware of a single secret foe; the Sages, who bind the whole world by a single word, will guard, with unremitting caution, against the enemy, lust.
(pa-u) puṟappakai kōṭiyiṉ mikku uṟiṉum añcār - veḷippakai kōṭikku mēlāka iruppiṉum payappaṭātavar; akappakai oṉṟu añcik kāppar - uṭpakai oṉṟiruntālum (ataṟkup) payantu kāval ceyvar; ulaku aṉaittum col oṉṟil yāppār - ulakam muḻuvataiyum oru collāl kaṭṭavalla muṉivar; palkālum kāmappakai purintu ōmpik kāppa - palamuṟaiyum kāmamākiya uṭpakaiyai mikavum taṭuttuk kāval ceyvar.
(ka-u) puṟap pakaivariṉum akappakaivar āñcutaṟkuriyar.
vañcakar naṭpai viṭavēṇṭum
puṟanaṭ ṭakamvērppār naccup pakaimai
veḷiyiṭṭu vēṟātal vēṇṭum - kaḻiperum
kaṇṇōṭṭam ceyyēl karuviyiṭ ṭāṟṟuvār
puṇvaittu mūṭār potintu. 56
56. After discovering the venomous enmity of those, who burn with inward hate, while they are externally friendly; instantly separate from them; treat them not with too much delicacy; they, who use the knife to cure a sore, will not close it over unhealed.
(pa-u) puṟam naṭṭu akam vērpār naccu pakaimai veḷi iṭṭu vēṟātal vēṇṭum - veḷiyē naṭpukkoṇṭu uḷḷē kōpittiruppa varuṭaiya nañcupōṉṟa pakaiyai veḷiyākki avaraip pirital vēṇṭum; kaḻiperum kaṇṇōṭṭam ceyyēl - (avarkaḷiṭattil) mikunta irakkaṅkoḷḷutalākātu; puṇ karuvi iṭṭu āṟṟuvār - puṇṇaik kattiyāl aṟuttu āṟṟuvār; potintu mūṭār - ataṉai maṟaittu mūṭār.
(ka-u) veḷiyē naṭpuk kāṭṭi uḷḷē pakai uṭaiyavarukku mukam pārttalākātu (tāṭcaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭalākātu).
vañcaka naṭpu
naṭpiṭaik kuyyamvait teyyā viṉaicūḻntu
vaṭkār tiṟattarāy niṉṟārkkut - tiṭpamām
nāḷulanta taṉṟē naṭuvaṉ naṭuviṉmai
vāḷā kiṭappaṉ maṟantu 57
57. For those, who, meeting friendship with treachery, secretly devise evil, and array themselves on the side of enemies; surely the appointed day of power has expired; and Yaman must be lying carelessly forgetful of their iniquity.
(pa-u) naṭpu iṭai kuyyam vaittu - naṇpariṭattu vañcakam vaittu; eyyā viṉai cūḻntu - (avar) aṟiyātapaṭi tīṅku ceyyac camayam pārttu; vaṭkār tiṟattarāy niṉṟārkku - pakaivar pakkattavarāy niṟpavarukku; tiṭpam ām nāḷ ulantatu - niccayamāy (avarkaḷiṉ) valiya vāḻnāḷ muṭintatu; naṭuvaṉ naṭuvuiṉmai maṟantu vāḷā kiṭappaṉ - iyamaṉ (avarkaḷiṉ) tīcceyalkaḷai maṟantu cummāvā iruppāṉ? irāṉ (iyamaṉ taṇṭippāṉ eṉṟavāṟu).
(ka-u) naṇpar pōliruntu vañcakam ceypavar maṟumaiyil naraka taṇṭaṉai āṭaivar.
maṉam kuṟṟāmaṉāl ellām kuṟṟamākat tōṉṟum
maṉatta kaṟuppeṉiṉ nalla ceyiṉum
aṉaittevaiyun tīyavē yākum - eṉaittuṇaiyum
tīyavē ceyyiṉum nallavāk kāṇpavē
māciṉ maṉatti ṉavar. 58
58. Where there is a stain on the heart, even good deeds will be all received as evil; but those of spotless minds, though men do them altogether evil, will still look upon it as good.
(pa-u) maṉatta kaṟuppu eṉiṉ - (oruvaṉukku) maṉattil kuṟṟam iruntāl; nalla ceyiṉum - (avaṉukku) nallavaṟṟaip piṟar ceytālum; aṉaittu evaiyum tīyavē ākum - avai ellām tīyaṉavē yākum; mācu il maṉattiṉavar - kuṟṟamillāta maṉattiṉaiyuṭaiyavarkku; eṉai tuṇaiyum tīyavē ceyyiṉum nallavā kāṇpavē - evvaḷavu tīyavaṟṟaic ceytālum avar avaṟṟai naṟceykaikaḷākavē koḷvar.
(ka-u) piṟar nalla kāriyaṅkaḷait taṉakkuc ceytālum keṭṭa maṉamuṭaiyavaṉ avaṟṟait tīmaiyāka karutuvaṉ.
nallavar kaṭuñcollum iṉiyatākum
iṉiyava reṉcoliṉum iṉcollē iṉṉār
kaṉiyum moḻiyuṅ kaṭuvē - aṉalkoḷuntum
veṅkāram veyteṉiṉum nōytīrkkummeypoṭippac
ciṅki kuḷirntuṅ kolum. 59
59. Whatever those of kind disposition may say, will still be sweet; but even the honied words of the unkind will be gall. The kindling borax, though it burn, will assuage pain. Arsenic, though it causes the body to shiver with cold, will kill.
(pa-u) iṉiyavar eṉ coliṉum iṉ collē - nallavar (kaṭiya) coṟkaḷaic coṉṉālum iṉiya collēyākum; iṉṉār kaṉiyum moḻiyum kaṭuvē - keṭṭavaruṭaiya iṉiya moḻiyum nañcākum; aṉal koḷuntum veṅkāram veytu eṉiṉum nōy tīrkkum - neruppup pōla (ericcalaik koṭukkum) veṅkāram erivukoṭuppatāyirunta pōtum nōyai āṟṟum; ciṅki mey poṭippa kuḷirntum kolum - nañcu mayir cilirkkumpaṭi kuḷircciyaik koṭuttuk koṉṟuviṭum.
(ka-u) nallavar veṟukkattakka coṟkaḷaic coṉṉālum avai iṉiyaṉavākum.
maṉam aḻukku aṟṟiruttalē nalla oḻukkam
poykuṟaḷai vaṉcol payaṉilaveṉ ṟinnāṉkum
eytāmai colliṉ vaḻukkāttu - meyyiṟ
pulamaintuṅ kāttu maṉamā cakaṟṟum
nalamaṉṟē nallā ṟeṉal 60
60. That virtue, which, by excluding these four, falsehood, slander, rude words, and unprofitable conversation; avoids offence in speech; and, by guarding the five organs of sense, keeps clear of the pollution of the heart; may indeed by termed the right way.
(pa-u) poy kuṟaḷai vaṉcol payaṉila eṉṟa in nāṉkum eytāmai - poy puṟaṅkūṟal kaṭuñcol payaṉillāc col eṉṉum inta nālum uṇṭākāmal; colliṉ vaḻukāttu - coṟkaḷiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷai nīkki; meyyil pulam aintum kāttu - uṭampil aimpulaṉ kaḷaiyum aṭakki; maṉam mācu akaṟṟum nalam aṉṟē nal āṟu eṉal - maṉattil kuṟṟattaip pōkkum naṟceyalaṉṟō nallavaḻi eṉṟu collappaṭuvatu.
(ka-u) maṉam aḻukkaṟṟu aimpulaṉkaḷaiyum aṭakkiyiruttalē nallavaḻi.
puli pacittālum pullait tiṉṉātu
nallā ṟoḻukkiṉ ṟalaitiṉṟār nalkūrntum
allaṉa ceytaṟ koruppaṭār - palpoṟiya
ceṅkaṭ puliyē ṟaṟappacittut tiṉṉāvām
paiṅkaṭ puṉattapaiṅ kūḻ. 61
61. Those, who have persisted in the course of the path of virtue, though in poverty, will not consent to do evil: the spotted red-eyed tiger, though ravenous with hunger, will not feed on the growing crop in the green meadow.
(pa-u) nal āṟu oḻukkiṉ talai niṉṟār - nalla oḻukka vaḻiyil ciṟantu oḻukupavar; nalkūrntum allaṉa ceyvataṟku oruppaṭār - vaṟumai yaṭaintaviṭattum tīyavaṟṟaic ceyya niṉaikka māṭṭār; pal poṟiya ceṅkaṇ puli ēṟu - palavarikaḷaiyum civanta kaṇkaḷaiyumuṭaiya āṇ puli; aṟa pacittum - mikap pacittatāyiṉum; paiṅkaṇ puṉatta paiṅ kūḻ tiṉṉāvām - paciya kollaiyiluḷḷa paciya payirait tiṉṉātu.
(ka-u) vaṟumai vantālum nallavar takāta ceyalkaḷaic ceyya niṉaiyār.
oḻukkattaip poruḷukku viṟṟalākātu
kulamviṟṟuk koḷḷum veṟukkaiyum vāymai
nalamviṟṟuk koḷḷun tiruvum - tavamviṟṟāṅ
kūṉōmpum vāḻvum urimaiviṟ ṟuṇpatūum
tāṉōmpik kāttital talai. 62
62. The riches gotten by selling the privileges of descent; the prosperity acquired by selling the benefit of truth; the ease that pampers the body at the expenses of religious duties; and the subsistence derived from selling the ties of friendship and kindred; to beware of all these is chief excellence.
(pa-u) kulam viṟṟu koḷḷum veṟukkaiyum - oruvaṉ taṉatu kulattai viṟṟup peṟum celvamum; vāymai nalam viṟṟu koḷḷum tiruvum - meyyiṉāl varum naṉmaiyai viṟṟup peṟum celvamum; tavam viṟṟu ūṉ ōmpum vāḻvum; viratattaik kaiviṭṭut taṉatu uṭalai vaḷarkkum vāḻkkaiyum - urimai viṟṟu uṇpatum - naṇpar uṟaviṉar mutaliya urimaikaḷaik kaiviṭṭu uṇṭu vāḻtalum; tāṉ ōmpik kāttal talai-tāṉ mikavum kāttal ciṟanta aṟamākum.
(ka-u) kulam mey tavam mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum uṟaviṉaraiyum poruḷukkākak kaiviṭutalākātu.
tīyavaḻiyil vanta poruḷ nillātu
iṭaiterin taccuṟuttu vañcit teḷiyār
uṭaimaikoṇ ṭēmāppār celvam - maṭanallār
pommaṉ mulaipōṟ paruttiṭiṉum maṟṟavar
nuṇṇiṭaipōṟ ṟēyntu viṭum. 63
63. The wealth of those, who exult in having gotten the possessions of the poor, by watching an opportunity to terrify and deceive them; though it increase like the swelling bosom of beautiful women, will quickly diminish like their slender waist.
(pa-u) iṭaiterintu - camayam pārttu; accuṟuttu - payamuṟuttal; vañcittu - kapaṭañceytu; eḷiyār uṭaimai koṇṭu ēmāppār celvam - eḷiyavarkaḷuṭaiya poruḷaik kavarntu cerukkaṭai pavaruṭaiya celvam; maṭam nallār pommaṉ mulaipōl paruttiṭiṉum - aḻakiya peṇkaḷiṉ periya taṉaṅkaḷ pōl (orupōtu) peritā yiruntapōtilum; avar nuṇṇiṭai pōl tēyntuviṭum - (ac celvam) appeṇkaḷiṉ melliya iṭai pōlak kuṟaintupōm.
(ka-u) ēḻaikaḷai vañcittup peṟṟa poruḷ viraivil aḻintu pōm.
uḷḷattil tirupti yaṭaiyāmai
peṟṟa ciṟukap peṟāta perituḷḷum
ciṟṟuyirk kākkam aritammā - muṟṟum
varavara vāymaṭuttu valvirāy māya
eritaḻaṉ māyā tirā. 64
64. How impossible is it for mortals to prosper, who, making little of what they have gotten, think much of what they have not; the wild fire, which devours everything as it comes, cannot but die away, as the fuel is consumed.
(pa-u) peṟṟa ciṟuka - tamakkuk kiṭaittavai (poruḷ) ciṟitākat tōṉṟa; peṟāta peritu uḷḷum ciṟṟuyirkku - kiṭaiyātavaṟṟaip peritu virumpum ciṟiya vāḻnāḷuṭaiya uyirkaḷukku; ākkam aritu - mēlum celvattāl vaḷarcciyaṭaital aritākum; eri taḻal varavara vāy maṭuttu - erikiṉṟa neruppuk kiṭaikkak kiṭaikka vāyiṭattē koṇṭu; val virāy muṟṟum māya māyātu irā - valiya viṟakukaḷ muṟṟum aḻiya erikiṉṟa taḻal aḻiyātirukkamāṭṭātu.
(ka-u) kiṭaittatōṭu tirupti aṭaiyātu pērācai koḷvōr peṟṟa poruḷāl aṉupavikkum payaṉaiyum iḻappar.
piṟar poruḷai virumpātavarukkup poruḷ cērum
tattam nilaikkuṅ kuṭimaikkun tappāmē
otta kaṭappāṭṭil tāḷūṉṟi - eyttum
aṟaṅkaṭaiyiṟ cellār piṟaṉporuḷum veḵkār
puṟaṅkaṭaiya tākum poruḷ. 65
65. To such as do not fail of what is due to their respective station and lineage; exert themselves in their proper calling; and though in difficulties, neither transgress the bounds of virtue, nor cover their neighbour’s wealth; riches will flow to their very gates.
(pa-u) tattam nilaikkum kuṭimaikkum tappāmē - tattamakkuriya takutikkum kuṭip piṟappukkum tavaṟātu; otta kaṭappāṭṭil tāḷūṉṟi - ēṟṟa muṟaiyil muyaṉṟu; eyttum aṟam kaṭaiyil cellār - maṟantum pāvavaḻiyil cellāmal; piṟaṉ poruḷum veḵkār - piṟaruṭaiya poruḷaiyum ācaippaṭātavaruṭaiya; puṟaṅkaṭaiyatu ākum poruḷ - talaivāyiliṭattē poruḷ tāṉē vantu cērum.
(ka-u) piṟar poruḷukku ācaippaṭātu nallavaḻiyil muyalpavarukkup poruḷ eḷitiṟ kiṭaikkum.
uyarntavar celvam palarkkum payaṉpaṭum
potumakaḷē pōlum talaiyāyār celvam
kulamakaḷē ēṉaiyōr celvam - kalaṉaḻinta
kaimmaiyār peṇmai nalampōṟ kaṭaiyāyār
celvam payaṉpaṭuva til. 66
66. The wealth of the eminently generous, is like a prostitute; the wealth of the middling class is like a chaste married woman; but the wealth of misers, like the charms of unadorned widows, produces no benefit.
(pa-u) talaiyāyār celvam potumakaḷē pōlum - talaimaiyāṉavaruṭaiya celvam potumakaḷir pōla ellōrukkum payaṉpaṭuvatākum; ēṉaiyōr celvam kulamakaḷē - iṭaiyāṉōr celvam kulamakaḷaippōlat taṉṉai yuṭaiyavaṉukkē payaṉpaṭuva tākum; kaṭaiyāyār celvam kalaṉaḻinta kaimmaiyār peṇmai nalam pōl payaṉpaṭuvatu il - kīḻmakkaḷiṉ celvam āparaṇaṅkaḷaik kaḷainta kaimpeṇṇaip pōla yārkkum payaṉpaṭātu.
(ka-u) nallavariṭattuḷḷa poruḷ ellārukkum payaṉpaṭum; kīḻ makkaḷiṭattuḷḷa poruḷ yārkkum payaṉpaṭātu.
koṭātavaṉ celvattilum vaṟumaiyē nallatu
vaḷḷaṉmai yillātāṉ celvattiṉ maṟṟaiyōṉ
nalkuravē pōlum naṉinalla - koṉṉē
aruḷilaṉ aṉpilaṉ kaṇṇaṟaiyaṉ eṉṟu
palarāl ikaḻap paṭāṉ. 67
67. Even the poverty of the liberal is far better than a miser’s wealth; for the former is not gratuitously subject to the opporbrious reproaches of the world, as ungracious, selfish, and pitiless.
(pa-u) vaḷḷaṉmai illātāṉ celvattiṉ - koṭukkum kuṇamillātavaṉuṭaiya celvattaik kāṭṭilum; maṟṟaiyōṉ nalkuravē naṉinalla - īyum kuṇamuṭaiyavaṉiṉ vaṟumaiyē mikavum nallatu; (ēṉeṉṟāl); aruḷilaṉ aṉpilaṉ kaṇṇaṟaiyaṉ eṉṟu palarāl koṉṉē ikaḻappaṭāṉ - irakkimillātavaṉ aṉpillātavaṉ, mukam pārātavaṉ eṉṟu palarāl vīṇē ikaḻappaṭamāṭṭāṉ.
(ka-u) koṭukkum kuṇamillāta celvarai ulakattavar iḻittuk kūṟuvar.
koṭukka iyalāviṭiṉum iṉcol vēṇṭum
īkai yariteṉiṉum iṉcoliṉum nalkūrtal
ōō koṭitu koṭitammā - nākoṉṟu
tīviṉaik kammiyaṉāl vāyppūṭ ṭiṭappaṭiṉmaṟ
ṟāvā vivareṉcey vār. 68
68. It may be impossible to give; but how cruel is it to be at the same time poor of kind words; yet what else can they do; if, as by a smith, their tongues be pierced through, and their mouths locked up by the evil deeds of a former life?
(pa-u) īkai aritu eṉiṉum; koṭuttal muṭiyātāyiṉum; iṉcoliṉum nalkūrtal ōō koṭitu koṭitu ammā - iṉiya col kūṟuvatilum vaṟumai aṭaital aiyō mikak koṭiyatākum; tīviṉaik kammiyaṉāl nā koṉṟu vāyppūṭṭu iṭappaṭiṉ ivar ā ā eṉ ceyvār - tīviṉai eṉṉum kammāḷaṉāl nāvaik kutti vāyppūṭṭup pōṭappaṭṭiruppatāl avarkaḷ vēṟu eṉṉa ceyvārkaḷ.
(ka-u) koṭukkāviṭiṉum iṉcol collavēṇṭum.
col vaṉmaiyaip payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum
colvaṉmai yuṇṭeṉiṟ koṉṉē viṭuttoḻital
nalviṉai kōṟaliṉ vēṟalla - vallaittam
ākkaṅ keṭuva tuḷateṉiṉum añcupavō
vākkiṉ payaṉkoḷ pavar. 69
69. Where a man’s words possess influence without cause to neglect to avail himself of it, is nothing else than to destroy the effect of good works; even when their wealth is threatened with immediate ruin, should those fear, who possess the advantages of eloquence?
(pa-u) colvaṉmai uṇṭu eṉiṉ - oruvaṉukkut taṉatu collukku valimai uṇṭāṉāl; koṉṉē viṭuttu oḻital nalviṉai kōṟaliṉ vēṟalla - (ataṉaip payaṉpaṭuttātu) vīṇē viṭṭiruttal nalla kāriyaṅkaḷai aḻittaliṉ vēṟu ākamāṭṭātu; vākkiṉ payaṉkoḷvār vallai tam ākkam keṭuvatu uḷatu eṉiṉum añcupavō - colliṉ valimaiyāl payaṉaṭaiya vallavar viraivil tamatu poruḷellām keṭṭup pōvatāyiruntālum ataṟkup payantu tamatu colvaṉmaiyaip payaṉpaṭuttātu viṭuvarō?
(ka-u) colvaṉmaiyuṭaiyavar ataṉaip payaṉpaṭutti ataṉālām payaṉai aṭaital vēṇṭum.
periyōr aṟaṉallā vaḻiyil poruḷīṭṭār
ciṟumuyaṟci ceytāṅ kuṟupayaṉ koḷḷap
peṟumeṉiṟ ṟāḻvarō tāḻār - aṟaṉalla
eṇmaiya vāyiṉuṅ kaiviṭ ṭariteṉiṉum
ōṇmaiyiṟ ṟīrntoḻuka lār. 70
70. When they can procure a great advantage by a little exertion, shall they shrink from it; who abandon what is contrary to virtue though it be easy, to pursue decidedly the path of duty, however difficult?
(pa-u) aṟaṉ alla oṇmaiyiṉ tīrntu oḻukalār - taruma mallā vaḻiyil vilakicceṉṟu oḻukātavar; ciṟu muyaṟci ceytu āṅku uṟupayaṉ koḷḷap peṟum eṉiṉ - ciṟiya muyaṟciyaic ceytu periya payaṉai aṭaiyakkūṭumāṉāl; eṇmaiya vāyiṉum kaiviṭṭu aritu eṉiṉum tāḻvarō - ceytaṟku eḷimaiyuṭaiya vāyiṉum aṟattaiviṭṭuk kaṭumai yāṉavaṟṟaic ceyyat tuṇivarō? tāḻār - tuṇiyār.
(ka-u) ciṟiya muyaṟciyāl periya poruḷ varumāyiṉum aṟaneṟiyil oḻukuvōr avaṟṟaic ceyyat tuṇiyār.
ceyya muṭiyātatai ceyya muṭiyumeṉak kūṟum puṉmai
ceyyakkaṭava allaṉavuñ ceytumaṉ ṉeṉpār
nayattaku nākarika meṉṉām - ceyirtturaippiṉ
neñcunōm eṉṟu talaitumippāṉ ṟaṇṇaḷipōl
eñcā teṭutturaikkaṟ pāṟṟu. 71
71. Of what avail is the flattering politeness of men, who forwardly profess that they will do things, which are not in their power to do? It may be fitly compared to the tender feelings of a man, who lest harsh words should grieve the heart of another, would cut off his head.
(pa-u) ceya kaṭava allaṉavum ceytumaṉ eṉpār - ceyyataṟ karumaiyuṭai kāriyaṅkaḷai mikutiyum ceytu muṭippōm eṉṟu colli niṟpavarkaḷatu; nayattaku nākarikam eṉṉām - virumpattakka mukamaṉ pēccu eṉṉa payaṉaic ceyyum; (icceyal etupōleṉil) ceyirttu uraippiṉ neñcu nēram eṉṟu - kōpittuc coṉṉāl maṉam varuntumeṉṟu; talai tumippāṉ taṇ aḷipōl eñcātu eṭuttu uraikkaṟpāṟṟu - talaiyai veṭṭupavaṉatu karuṇai pōṉṟateṉṟu virittuk kūṟattakkatākum.
(ka-u) tāṉ ceyya muṭiyāta karumattaic ceyya muṭiyumeṉṟu colpavariṉ ceyal payaṉaṟṟatu.
valiyaṟṟavar payaṉaṟṟaṉa ceyyiṉum āravārippar
allaṉa ceyyiṉum ākulaṅ kūḻākkoṇ
ṭolkātār vāyviṭ ṭulampupa - vallār
piṟarpiṟar ceypapōṟ ceytakka ceytāṅ
kaṟimaṭam pūṇṭuniṟpā rār. 72
72. Many, to reap a harvest of empty sound, scruple not to boast aloud, though they have done nothing; on the contrary, where are the men, who, when they have done a good action, assume feigned ignorance, as if others had done it?
(pa-u) ollātār - valiyaṟṟavar; allaṉa ceyyiṉum ākulam kūḻ ā koṇṭu vāy viṭṭu ulampum - payaṉallātavaṟṟaic ceytālum āravārattaiyē payaṉākakkoṇṭu vāyviṭṭu āravārañ ceyvar; vallār piṟar - valiya piṟar; piṟar ceypapōl ceytakka ceytu - piṟarāl ceyyappaṭuvaṉa pōṉṟa ceyyattakka ceyalkaḷaic ceytu; āṅku aṟimaṭam pūṇṭu niṟpār - avviṭattil aṟiyāmaiyuṭaiyavarpōl aṭakkamāka iruppar.
(ka-u) ariyavaṟṟaic ceyya māṭṭātavar aṭakkamiṉṟi ārāvārañ ceytiruppar.
piṟar paḻiyai eṭuttut tūṟṟuvāṉ tiṟam
pakaiyiṉṟu pallār paḻiyeṭut tōti
nakaiyoṉṟē naṉpayaṉāk koḷvāṉ - payamiṉṟu
meyvitirppuk kāṇpāṉ koṭiṟuṭaittuk kolvāṉpōl
kaivitirt tañcap paṭum. 73
73. A man who, without malice, takes up, and dwells upon the scandal of others, for the solitary advantage of exciting laughter, will be feared and trembled at, like a man, who, without any benefit to himself, would kill his neighbour by a blow on the cheek, that the might see the body quiver in death.
(pa-u) pakai iṉṟu pallār paḻi eṭuttu ōti - oru pakaiyum illātirukkavum palarukkuḷḷa vacaikaḷai eṭuttut tūṟṟi; nakai oṉṟē nal payaṉāk koḷvāṉ - nakaiyāṭutalaiyē nalla payaṉ eṉṟu koḷpavaṉ; payamiṉṟu meyvitirppuk kāṇpāṉ koṭiṟu uṭaittuk kolvāṉpōl añcappaṭum - yātum payaṉillāmal uṭampil uṇṭākum naṭukkattaik kāṇutaṟ poruṭṭu piṟaruṭaiya kaṉṉattai uṭaippavaṉaippōla añcat takkavaṉāvāṉ.
(ka-u) pakaiyillātirukkavum piṟar paḻikaḷē eṭuttut tūṟṟi nakaiyāṭukiṉṟavaṉai añcutal vēṇṭum.
teyvam niṉṟu oṟukkum
teyva muḷateṉpār tīya ceyappukiṉ
teyvamē kaṇṇiṉṟu niṉṟoṟukkum - teyvam
ilateṉpārk killaittam iṉputalvark kaṉṟē
palakālum colvār payaṉ. 74
74. If those, who confess the existence of God, enter on an evil action, God will stay and rebuke them without mercy; not those, who say, “there is no God”. Is it not to their own dear children, that men patiently repeat instruction?
(pa-u) teyvam uḷatu eṉpār tīya ceya pukiṉ - teyvam uṇṭu eṉkiṉṟavar tīya ceyalkaḷaic ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉāl; teyvamē kaṇ niṉṟu oṟukkum-atteyvamē kaṇṇukku muṉṉāl niṉṟu taṇṭikkum; teyvam ilatu eṉpārkku illai - teyvam illai eṉṟu collu pavarkku avvāṟu teyvam taṇṭippatillai; iṉ putalvarkku aṉṟē palakālum payaṉ colvār - tamatu iṉiya putalvarkaḷuk kallavō (peṟṟōr) payaṉtarakkūṭiya puttamatikaḷaic colluvar.
(ka-u) kaṭavuḷ uṇṭeṉpavar tīyavaḻiyil ceṉṟāl kaṭavuḷ taṇṭittu avarai nalla vaḻiyil celuttuvar.
nalvaḻiyil varātaporuḷ tīmai viḷaikkum
tīya ceyaṟceyvā rākkam perukiṉum
tīyaṉa tīyaṉavē vēṟalla - tīyaṉa
nallaṉa ākāvām nāviṉ puṟanakkik
kolluṅ kavayamāp pōl. 75
75. Though the prosperity of those who do evil, increase; still evil is but evil; evil will never be good. Like the wild cow, which licks the body with her tongue, so as to create pleasing sensation, while at the same time she thereby causes death.
(pa-u) tīya ceyal ceyvār ākkam perukiṉum - keṭṭa kāriyaṅ kaḷaic ceypavariṉ celvam vaḷarvatāṉālum; tīyaṉa - tīya vaḻiyil vanta accelvaṅkaḷ; nā iṉpuṟa nakki kollum kavayamāpōl - nāviṉāl iṉpam tarumāṟu nakkip piṉ kolkiṉṟa kāṭṭup pacuvaip pōla; tīyaṉavē vēṟalla - tīyavaiyallāmal vēṟalla; tīyaṉa nallaṉa ākā - tīyavai nallavai ākamāṭṭā.
(ka-u) tīya ceyalāl vantaporuḷ tīmaiyaiyē viḷaikkum.
paḻi nāṇuvār uyarntavarāvar
naṉmakkaḷ cennāt taḻumpirukka nāḷvāyum
cenneṟic celvāriṟ kīḻallar - muṉṉaittam
ūḻvali yuṉṉip paḻināṇi yuḷḷuṭaivār
tīya ceyiṉuñ cila. 76
76. Not inferior to those who walk every day in the right way, so as to gain the approbation of good men; are the men who though they may do some things wrong, are broken in spirit at the thought of the force of destiny, and with the shame of reproach.
(pa-u) tam muṉṉai ūḻvali uṉṉi - tām muṟpiṟappiṟ ceyta tamatu ūḻiṉ valiyāl (tīviṉai ceyya nērntatai) niṉaittu; paḻi nāṇi uḷḷuṭaivār - (varum) paḻic collukku añci maṉam varuntuvōr; cila tīya ceyiṉum - tīyavai cilavaṟṟaic ceytālum; (avarkaḷ) naṉmakkaḷ cem nā taḻumpu irukka nāḷvāyum cenneṟi ceyvāriṉ - nallōr (pala muṟaiyum avar naṉmaiyai eṭuttuc collutalāl) nāttaḻum pēṟumpaṭi tiṉamum nalloḻukkattil celpavarilum pārkka; kīḻallar - tāḻntavarākamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) tām ceyta kuṟṟattukkāka varuntum iyalpiṉar naṉṉeṟiyil oḻukum uyarntavarukkut tāḻntavarākamāṭṭār.
piṟaṉ maṉai nayattal
piṟaṉvarai niṉṟāḷ kaṭaittalaic cēṟal
aṟaṉaṉṟē āyiṉu māka - ciṟuvaraiyum
taṉṉalatta tāyiṉuṅ koḷka nalamaṉṟē
meynnaṭuṅka vuṇṇaṭuṅku nōy. 77
77. To visit the gate of a neighbour’s wife is certainly not virtue. But suppose it to be so. Then if it yield even a brief enjoyment, be it allowed; but surely the torture, that shakes the body and the mind with agitation, is not enjoyment.
(pa-u) piṟaṉ varai niṉṟāḷ kaṭaittalai cēṟal - piṟaṉuṭaiya ellaikkuḷ niṟpavaḷākiya oruvaṉ maṉaiviyiṉ talaivāyi liṭattuc cellutal; aṟaṉ aṉṟu - nalla oḻukkamaṉṟu; āyiṉum ākuka - appaṭi nallatu allāviṭiṉum ākuka; ciṟu varaiyum nalattatu āyiṉ koḷka - ciṟitaḷavāyiṉum iṉpam taruvatāyiṉ ataṉaik kaikkoḷka; nalam aṉṟē - āṉāl atu iṉpan taruvataṉṟu; mey naṭuṅka maṉam naṭuṅkum nōy - uṭal naṭuṅka maṉamum naṭuṅkat tuṉpan taruvatākum.
(ka-u) piṟaṉ maṉaiyāḷai virumputal tuṉpan taruvatākum.
iṉpam tuyttal
karumañ citaiyāmē kalvi keṭāmē
tarumamun tāḻvu paṭāmē - perituntam
iṉṉalamuṅ kuṉṟāmē yēriḷaṅ kompaṉṉār
naṉṉalan tuyttal nalam. 78
78. Without interruption to business; without ruining learning without preventing charity; and without greatly diminishing our household goods; the enjoyment of the charms of those who resemble a beautiful young branch, may be considered good.
(pa-u) karumam citaiyāmē - tām ceyyum kāriyam keṭāmalum; kalvi keṭāmē - paṭippuk keṭāmalum; tarumamum tāḻvu paṭāmē - tarumamum kuṟaivu varāmal; peritum tam ilnalamum kuṉṟāmē - peritum tamatu vīṭṭu naṉmaikaḷum keṭāmal; ēr iḷa kompu aṉṉār nal nalam tuyttal talai - aḻakiya iḷam kompiṉai yotta tammaṉaiviyariṉ nalla iṉpattai aṉupavittal nallatu.
(ka-u) taṉatu kaṭamaikaḷukkup paṅkam varāmal maṉaiviyōṭu cērntu iṉpam nukartal vēṇṭum.
kāmattiṉ ceyal
kolaiyañcāhr poynnāṇār māṉamum ōmpār
kaḷavoṉṟō ēṉaiyavuñ ceyvār - paḻiyōṭu
pāvamiḵ teṉṉār piṟituṟ ṟeṉceyyār
kāmaṅ katuvappaṭ ṭār. 79
79. They fear not murder; they are not ashamed of falsehood; they preserve not reputation; they rob not in one way only, but in all; they think not of any act that it is sin attended with infamy; what else will they not do, who have fallen into the power of lust?
(pa-u) kāmam katuvappaṭṭār - kāmattāl kavarappaṭṭavar; kolai añcār - kolai ceyvataṟkup payappaṭār; poy nāṇār - poy colla veṭkappaṭār; māṉamum ōmpār - tamatu mariyātaiyaiyum pātukākkamāṭṭār; kaḷavu oṉṟō - kaḷavuceytal oṉṟu māttiramō; ēṉaiyavum ceyvār - mēlum pāvaṅkaḷaiyum ceyvār; iḵtu paḻiyoṭu pāvam eṉṉār - entat tīya ceyalaiyum paḻiyum pāvamumuṭaiyatu eṉṟu niṉaiyār; piṟitu eṉ ceyyār - vēṟuyātu ceyyamāṭṭār (ellāp pāvaṅkaḷum ceyvar)
(ka-u) kāmattiṉ vacappaṭṭār ellāp pāvaṅkaḷaiyum ceyya añcamāṭṭār.
iṉippai virumpātu kaippai virumpu miyalpu
tiruviṉu nallāḷ maṉaikkiḻatti yēṉum
piṟarmaṉaikkē pīṭaḻintu niṟpar - naṟuviya
vāyiṉa vēṉum umiḻntu kaṭuttiṉṉum
tīya vilaṅkiṉ cila. 80
80. There are some, who though their own wife he more beautiful than Lachmi; nevertheless degrade themselves by hankering after their neighbour’s; like an evil beast, which though it has sweet food in its mouth, will reject it to feed on bitter.
(pa-u) maṉaikkiḻatti tiruviṉum nallāḷ eṉiṉum - maṉaivi tirumakaḷilum aḻakuṭaiyavaḷāyiruntālum; naṟuviya vāyiṉa ēṉum umiḻntu - iṉiya poruḷ vāyiliruntaṉavāyiṉum avaṟṟaik kakkiviṭṭu; kaṭu tiṉṉum tīya vilaṅkiṉ cilar - kaippuḷḷa poruḷait tiṉṉum keṭṭa vilaṅkupōṉṟa cilar; piṟar maṉaikkē pīṭu aḻintu niṟpar - piṟaruṭaiya maṉaiyāḷiṉ cērkkaikkē tamatu perumai keṭṭu niṟpar.
(ka-u) tīyavar tamatu maṉaiviyar aḻakuṭaiyavarāyiruppiṉum piṟa peṇkaḷaiyē virumpuvar.
makkaṭpēṟē ciṟanta pēṟu
kaṟpuṭut taṉpu muṭittunāṇ meypūci
naṟkuṇa naṟceykai pūṇṭāṭku - makkaṭpē
ṟeṉpatō rākkamum uṇṭāyiṉ illaṉṟē
koṇṭāṟkuc ceytavam vēṟu. 81
81. If a wife clothed with chastity, crowned with affection, anointed with modesty, and adorned with the jewels of a good heart and life, possess only the wealth of child-bearing; what more can her husband have to seek by the performance of penance?
(pa-u) kaṟpu uṭuttu - kaṟpākiya āṭaiyai uṭuttu; aṉpu muṭittu - aṉpākiya pūvaic cūṭi; mey nāṇ pūci - uṭampil naṟumaṇamākiya cāntaip pūci; naṟkuṇam naṟceykai pūṇṭāṭku - naṟkuṇam naṟceykaikaḷai āparaṇamāka aṇintavaḷukku; makkaḷ pēṟu eṉpatōr celvam uṇṭāyiṉ - piḷḷaikaḷaip peṟṟiruttal eṉṉum celvam uṇṭāṉāl; koṇṭāṟku cey tavam vēṟu il - kaṇavaṉukku vēṟu ceyya vēṇṭiya tavam oṉṟumillai.
(ka-u) naṟkuṇa naṟceykaiyuḷḷa maṉaivikkup piḷḷaip pēṟu iruttal ciṟappu.
mātar maṉam
ēnteḻil mikkāṉ iḷaiyāṉ icaivallāṉ
kāntaiyar kaṇkavar nōkkattāṉ - vāynta
nayaṉuṭai iṉcollāṉ kēḷeṉiṉu mātark
kayalāṉmēl ākum maṉam. 82
82. Though their husband be of surpassing beauty, youthful, powerful in song, of an aspect to ravish the eyes of maidens, and uniting truth with courtesy in his pleasing address; the heart of women will still be fixed on others.
(pa-u) kēḷ ēntu eḻil mikkāṉ - kaṇavaṉ ciṟanta aḻakil uyarntavaṉ; iḷaiyāṉ - iḷamaipparuvamuṭaiyavaṉ; icaivallāṉ - irākam pāṭuvatil vallavaṉ; kāntaiyar kaṇkavar nōkkattāṉ - peṇkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷaik kavarum tōṟṟattavaṉ; vāynta nayaṉ uṭai iṉ collāṉ eṉiṉum - poruttamāṉa melliya iṉiya collaip pēcukiṉṟavaṉ āṉālum; mātarkku maṉam ayalār mēl ākum - peṇkaḷukku maṉam ayalavarmēl cellum.
(ka-u) kaṇavaṉ aḻaku naṟkuṇam mutaliyavaṟṟāl ciṟappuṭaiyavaṉā yiruppiṉum kaṟpillāta makaḷiriṉ maṉam ayal āṭavar mēṟ cellum.
kaṟpil makaḷir
kaṟpil makaḷiṉ nalamviṟ ṟuṇavukoḷḷum
poṟṟoṭi nallār naṉinallar - maṟṟuttaṅ
kēḷvarkkum ētilarkkum taṅkaṭkum taṅkiḷaiñar
yāvarkkum kēṭucū ḻār. 83
83. Far better than a wife without chasity, are the fair, with golden bracelets, who subsist by selling their favours; for they do not involve in misery their husband, their neighbour, themselves and all their connexions.
(pa-u) nalam viṟṟu uṇavu koḷḷum poṉtoṭi nallār - tamatu iṉpattaip piṟarukku viṟṟu uṇavu koḷkiṉṟa poṉvaḷai aṇinta peṇkaḷ; kaṟpil makaḷiriṉ naṉi nallar - kaṟpillāta peṇkaḷilum pārkka mika nallavarāvar; tam kēḷvarkkum - (muṉkūṟappaṭṭa vilaimātar) tamatu kātalarukkum; ētilarkkum - ayalavarkkum; taṅkaṭkum - taṅkaḷukkum; tam iḷaiñar yāvarkkum kēṭu cūḻār - tamatu uṟaviṉar evarukkum kēṭuceyya niṉaiyār.
(ka-u) kaṟpillā makaḷiriṉum potu makaḷir nallar.
kaṟpil makaḷir piṟappu
muṟaiyum kuṭimaiyum pāṉmaiyum nōkkār
niṟaiyum neṭunāṇum pēṇār - piṟitumoru
peṟṟimai pētaimaik kuṇṭē perumpāvam
kaṟpiṉ makaḷir piṟappu. 84
84. Regardless of their duty, of their birth and of propriety; insensible to shame, and respectability; what good quality is there compatible with the folly of frail women? The birth of such is the retribution for grievous sin.
(pa-u) muṟaiyum - tamakkum maṟṟavarkaḷukkumuḷḷa uṟavu muṟaikaḷaiyum; kuṭimaiyum - piṟar kuṭiyiṉ taṉmaiyaiyum; pāṉmaiyum nōkkār - tamakkum piṟarkkumuḷḷa takutiyaiyum pārār; niṟaiyum nāṇum pēṇār - maṉa uṟutippāṭṭaiyum nāṇattaiyum pātukākkamāṭṭār; pētaimaikku piṟitum oru peṟṟimai uṇṭē - aṟiyāmai koṇṭa peṇkaḷukku ivaiyallāmal vēṟu kuṇaṅkaḷumuṇṭō? kaṟpu il makaḷir piṟappu perum pāvam - kaṟpillāta peṇkaḷiṉ piṟappup periya pāvamākum.
(ka-u) kaṟpillāta peṇkaḷiṭattil nalla kuṇaṅkaḷ evaiyum kāṇappaṭamāṭṭā.
tuṟavikaḷ peṇkaḷaip pārār
peṇmai viyavār peyarum eṭuttōtār
kaṇṇoṭu neñcuṟaippa nōkkuṟār - paṇṇoṭu
pāṭal cevimaṭār paṇpalla pārāṭṭār
vīṭil pulappakai yiṉār. 85
85. They, who admire not female beauty; who dwell not in conversation on their names; who excite not the eye and heart with licentious gazes; who indulge not the ear in music and song, and commend not what is contrary to decency, are the men who wage successful war with the unrestrained general organs.
(pa-u) vīṭu il pulam pakaiyiṉār - aṭakkamuṭiyāta aimpulaṉ kaḷukkup pakaivarāyuḷḷa (aimpulaṉkaḷai aṭakkiya) tuṟavikaḷ; peṇmai viyavār - peṇkaḷiṉ nalla taṉmaikaḷaip pukaḻār; peyarum eṭuttu ōtār - peṇkaḷ eṉṟa peyarait tāṉum eṭuttuc collār; neñcu uṟaippa kaṇṇoṭu nōkku uṟār - neñcil avarkaḷiṉ eṇṇam paṭiyum paṭi kaṇṇālum pārār; paṇṇoṭu pāṭal cevi maṭār - icaiyōṭu pāṭappaṭum pāṭalkaḷaiyum (cevikoṭuttuk) kēḷār; paṇpu alla pārāṭṭār - nalla taṉmaikaḷallāta piṟavaṟṟai yum pārāṭṭamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) tuṟavikaḷ peṇkaḷaip pārkka māṭṭār; maṟṟum pulaṉkaḷaik kuḻappum ceyalkaḷil īṭupaṭamāṭṭār.
tuṟavikaḷ uṇṭi curukkuvar
tuyiṟcuvaiyum tūnallār tōṭcuvaiyu mellām
ayiṟcuvaiyi ṉākuvaveṉ ṟeṇṇi - ayiṟcuvaiyum
pittuṇṇāk koḷpapōṟ koḷpa piṟarcilarpōl
mottuṇā moympi ṉavar. 86
86. They, who, deeming indulgence in the sweets of sleep and the enjoyment of the charms of women of unsullied beauty, to be encouraged by indulgence in the sweets of feasting, take their food abstemiously as an idiot, are possessed of a strength which none may prevail against.
(pa-u) piṟar cilar pōl mottuṇā moyppiṉavar - (tuṟavika ḷallarāṉa) cilaraip pōla (aimpulaṉkaḷāl) aṭipaṭāta valiyaiyuṭaiya tuṟavikaḷ; tuyil cuvaiyum - nittirai iṉpamum, tūnallār tōḷ cuvaiyum ellām - aḻakiya peṇkaḷiṉ tōḷkaḷaic cērum cukamum ākiya ellām; ayil cuvaiyil ākuva eṉṟu eṇṇi - uṇavuc cukattiṉāl uṇṭākiṉṟavai eṉṟu niṉaittu; ayil cuvaiyum pittu uṇākoḷpa pōl koḷpa - uṇavuc cukattaiyum paittiyakkāraṉ uṇavu koḷvatu pōl koḷvar.
(ka-u) uṇavu mikutalāl maṟṟa iṉpaṅkaḷil viruppamuṇṭāku meṉṟu niṉaintu tuṟaviyar aṟpa uṇavu koḷvar.
tuṟavikaḷ uṭampaik kavaṉiyār
aṉpō ṭaruḷuṭaiya rēṉum uyirnilaimaṟ
ṟeṉpiyakkaṅ kaṇṭum puṟantarār - puṉpulāṟ
poykkuṭil ōmpuvarō pōtattāl tāmvēynta
pukkil kuṭipukutu vār. 87
87. Holy men, though of tender and benevolent disposition, do not turn back, though their bones show through the skin; should they who possess a heavenly mansion, which they have built for themselves by wisdom, seek to preserve a false tabernacle of rankly decaying flesh?
(pa-u) pōtattāl tām vēynta pukkil kuṭipukutuvār - meyaṟiviṉāl tām amaitta vīṭṭu ulakil cellum tuṟavikaḷ; puṉ pulāl poy kuṭil ōmpuvarō - iḻinta ūṉ mūṭiya kuṭicaiyaip pātukāpparō; aṉpōṭu aruḷ uṭaiyar ēṉum - avarkaḷ aṉpum irakkamum uṭaiyavarkaḷāṉālum; uyir nilai eṉpu iyakkaṅ kaṇṭum puṟantarār - uyirkku iruppiṭamāṉa uṭampu (melintu) elumpu acaiyak kaṇṭālum; puṟantarār - ataṟku iḷaikkamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) vīṭṭu iṉpattai vēṇṭupavar uṭampaip pēṇuvatiṟ kavalai koḷḷār.
maṟṟiṉpam vēṇṭuvār ciṟṟiṉpam virumpār
ciṟṟiṉpañ ciṉṉīra tāyiṉum aḵtuṟṟār
maṟṟiṉpam yāvaiyuṅ kaiviṭupa - muṟṟuntām
pēriṉpa mākkaṭa lāṭuvār vīḻpavō
pāriṉpap pāḻṅkumpi yil 88
88. Small as is sensual pleasure, its votaries renounce all other enjoyments; shall they who bathe in the great sea of heavenly delight, sink in the mire of earthly indulgence?
(pa-u) ciṟṟiṉpam cil nīratu āyiṉum - ciṟṟiṉpam ciṟu poḻutaiya iṉpamāyiṉum; aḵtu uṟṟār - avviṉpattaip peṟṟavar; maṟṟiṉpam yāvaiyum kaiviṭupa - maṟṟa ellā iṉpaṅkaḷaiyum viṭṭu viṭuvar; muṟṟum pēriṉpamā kaṭal āṭuvār tām - muṟṟāka (eppōtum) pēriṉpa mākiya periya kaṭalil mūḻkukiṉṟavar; pār iṉpa pāḻ kumpiyil vīḻpavō - ulaka iṉpamākiya koṭiya narakil viḻuvarō.
(ka-u) pēriṉpattil mūḻkukiṉṟavar ulaka iṉpaṅkaḷai virumpār.
peṇ cērkkai tavirttal
evviṉaiya rēṉum iṇaiviḻaiccoṉ ṟilleṉiṉ
tevvun ticainōkkik kaitoḻūum - avviṉai
kātta lilarēl eṉaittuṇaiya rāyiṉum
tūrttarun tūrppār avar. 89
89. Whatever be the conduct of those, who profess to have renounced the world, if they be only chaste, even their enemies will raise to them their hands in adoration. If however distinguished in other respects, they guard not this virute, even the profligate will not pass them ever uncensured.
(pa-u) evviṉaiyarēṉum - (tuṟavikaḷ) vēṟu evvakaiyāṉa ceyalaic ceypavarāyiruntālum; iṇai viḻaiccu oṉṟu il eṉil - peṇ cērkkai eṉṉum oṉṟu illātavarāyiṉ; tevvum ticai nōkki kai toḻum - (avar) pakaivarum avarirukkum ticaiyaip pārttuk kai yeṭuttu vaṇaṅkuvar; avviṉai kāttal ilarēl - (peṇ cērkkai illāmai eṉṉum) antac ceyalaik kāttuk koḷḷal illātava rāṉāl; eṉaittuṇaiyar āyiṉum - avarkaḷ vēṟu evvaḷavu ciṟanta varkaḷāyirunta pōtilum; tūrttarum avar tūrppār - keṭṭavarkaḷum avar mītu paḻi kūṟuvar.
(ka-u) peṇ cērkkaiyai virumpātiruttalē tuṟavikaḷukkuc ciṟappu.
naṟceyalkaḷil kālattaik kaḻittal vēṇṭum
paraparappi ṉōṭē palapala ceytāṅ
kiravupakal pāḻuk kiṟaippa - oruvāṟṟāṉ
nallāṟṟiṉ ūṅkiṟ pataṟik kulaikulaipa
evvāṟṟāṉ uyvār ivar. 90
90. There are some, who doing various things with bustling haste, will spend day and night in watering the wilderness; but if by any means, one turn them into the good way, they will be immediately startled with alarm. How shall such prosper?
(pa-u) palapala paraparappiṉōṭu ceytu iravu pakal pāḻukku iṟaippa - (palar) palavaṟṟai iravupakalāka viraintu ceytu kālattai vīṇē kaḻippar; oru āṟṟāṉ - oru vakaiyāl; nalāṟṟil ūkkiṉ meypataṟi kulai kulaipa - nalla vaḻiyil celuttiṉāl uṭal naṭuṅkip pataṟuvar; ivar evvāṟṟāṉ uyvār - ivarkaḷ evvaḻiyil taḻaippārkaḷ.
(ka-u) kālattai vīṇ ceyalkaḷiṟ kaḻikkātu naṟceyalil pōkka vēṇṭum.
iḷamaiyil tavam ceyka
iḷaiyam mututavam āṟṟutum nōṟṟeṉ
ṟuḷaiviṉṟu kaṇpāṭum ūḻē - viḷiviṉṟu
vāḻnāḷ varampuṭaimai kāṇparēl kāṇpārum
tāḻāmē nōṟpār tavam. 91
91. To sleep away our days in careless indolence, saying, “We are young, and may perform the duties of devotion in old age,” might be right, if we could see the limit of our lives; but those, who could do so, whould not relax in the performance of religious duties.
(pa-u) viḷivu iṉṟu vāḻnāḷ varampuṭaimai kāṇparēl - (iḷamaiyil) maraṇam illātiruppatākiya ellaiyai aṟintiruppā rāyiṉ; iḷaiyam - nām (ippōtu) iḷamaip paruvattiṉōm; mutu tavam nōṟṟu āṟṟutum eṉṟu - mutumaip paruvattil varuntit tavattaic ceyvōm eṉṟu niṉaittu; uḷaivu iṉṟu kaṇpāṭum ūḻē - varuttamillātu kaṇṇuṟaṅkiyiruppatum muṟaimaiyākum; kāṇpārum - (tamatu vāḻnāḷiṉ) ellaiyai aṟiyak kūṭiyavarum; tāḻāmē tavam nōṟpār - kālam pōkkāmal tavattaic ceyvar.
(ka-u) maraṇam eppōtu varumeṉṟu yārumaṟiyārātaliṉ viraintu tavañ ceytal vēṇṭum.
tīya ūḻāl nallaṉavum tīyavām
nallavai ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉum nōṉāmē
allaṉa allavaṟṟiṉ koṇṭuykkum - elli
viyaṉeṟic celvārai āṟalait tuṇpār
celavu piḻaittuyppa pōl. 92
92. Though a man may begin to do good, his evil destiny, without suffering it, will turn it into evil: as those who live by plundering on the highway, lead astray the traveller in the dark night.
(pa-u) nallaṉa ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉum ? nalla ceyalkaḷaic ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉālum; allaṉa - tīya viṉaippayaṉ; nōṉāmē allavaṟṟil koṇṭu uykkum - atil tarikka viṭātu ataṉaiyum tīya ceyalākki viṭum; elli āṟu alaittu uṇpār - iravilē vaḻippaṟi ceytu uṇpavar; viyaṉ neṟi celvārai - periya vaḻiyiṭattē celvōrai; celavu piḻaittu uyppa pōl - vaḻi tappac ceyvatu pōlākum.
tīya ūḻ uḷḷavar nalla kāriyaṅkaḷaic ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉālum avai tīya kāriyaṅkaḷāka muṭiyum.
poy vēṭattāṟ payaṉillai
neñcu puṟampāt tuṟantār tavappōrvai
kañcuka maṉṟu piṟitoṉṟē - kañcukam
eppulamuṅ kāvāmē meyppulaṅ kākkumaṟ(ṟu)
ippulamuṅ kāvā titu. 93
93. The cloak of devotion worn by those who have renounced the world externally, is some what different from a garment; for a garment though it guards not the other organs of senses, yet guards the body-the other guards not even this.
(pa-u) neñcu puṟampu ā tuṟantār tavap pōrvai - neñcil tuṟavātu vēṭattil tuṟantavaratu tava vēṭam; kañcukam aṉṟu piṟitu oṉṟē - atu caṭṭai pōṉṟataṉṟu vēṟoṉṟākum; kañcukam ep pulamum kāvāmē mey pulam kākkum - caṭṭai ellāp pulaṉkaḷaiyum kāvāviṭṭālum uṭampākiya pulaṉaik kākkum; itu ippulamum kāvātu - ittavavēṭam intappulaṉaiyum kākkamāṭṭātu.
(ka-u) maṉatil tuṟavātavaṉiṉ tavavēṭam payaṉaṟṟatu.
vañcittu oḻukutal
vañcit toḻukum matiyilikāḷ! yāvaraiyum
vañcittōm eṉṟu makiḻaṉmiṉ - vañcitta
eṅkum uḷaṉoruvaṉ kāṇuṅkol eṉṟañci
aṅkaṅ kulaiva taṟivu. 94
94. Ye fools, who live in the practice of deceit, exult not in the thought that you have succeeded in deceiving all. That is true wisdom, which will tremble at the awful thought, that there is one everywhere present, who will behold all deceit.
(pa-u) vañcittu oḻukum matiyilikāḷ - (piṟaraik) kapaṭañ ceytu vāḻum aṟivillātavarkaḷē; yāvaraiyum vañcittōm eṉṟu makiḻaṉmiṉ - ellāraiyum vañcittōm eṉṟu colli makiḻātīrkaḷ; vañcitta - nīvir vañcaṉai ceytatai; eṅkum uḷaṉ oruvaṉ kāṇum eṉṟu añci aṅkam kulaivatu aṟivu - eṅkum niṟaintavaṉākiya iṟaivaṉ kāṇpāṉ eṉappayantu uṭal naṭuṅkuvatu aṟivu ākum.
(ka-u) eṅkum niṟainta kaṭavuḷ ellāc ceyalkaḷaiyum aṟivārātaliṉ piṟarai vañcittōmeṉṟu makiḻavēṇṭām.
maṟaittuc ceyyiṉum paḻi veḷippaṭum
maṟaivaḻip paṭṭa paḻimoḻi teyvam
paṟaiyaṟaintāṅ kōṭip parakkum - kaḻimuṭaip
puṉpulāl nāṟṟam puṟampotintu mūṭiṉuñ
ceṉṟutaikkum cēyār mukattu. 95
95. The infamy of evil done in secret, will spread abroad as though God had proclaimed it to the beat of drum. Though the rank stench of putrid flesh be concealed and covered up, it will escape to penetrate the senses of far distant persons.
(pa-u) maṟai vaḻippaṭṭa paḻimoḻi - maṟaiviṟ ceyyappaṭṭa paḻic ceyalaip paṟṟiya (paḻi) moḻi; teyvam paṟai aṟaintu āṅku ōṭip parakkum - teyvam paṟai aṭittup parappiyatu pōla appoḻutē viraintu paravum; pul pulāl kaḻimuṭai nāṟṟam - iḻinta iṟaicciyiṉ mikak keṭṭa nāṟṟam; puṟam potintu mūṭiṉum cēyār mukattu ceṉṟu taikkum - mēlē potintu mūṭi vaittālum tūrattiluḷḷavar mukattil ceṉṟu tākkum.
(ka-u) maṟaiviṟ ceyyappaṭum paḻic ceyalkaḷ viraivil veḷivantuviṭum.
valiyāṟ keṭṭāl uyyār
meliyāṟ viḻiṉum oruvāṟṟāṉ uypa
valiyārmaṟ ṟoṉṟāṉum uyyār - nilaitappi
noyya caḻakkeṉa vīḻāvām vīḻiṉum
uyyumāl uyyā piṟa. 96
96. The weak, though they fall, will by some means survive; but the powerful survive not by any means; light things do not fall from their place with a loud crash; but if they should fall, they would be saved; not so the others.
(pa-u) meliyār vīḻiṉum oru āṟṟāṉ uypa - ciṟiyavar nilaitappi viḻuntālum oruvāṟu piḻaippar; valiyār maṟṟu oṉṟēṉum uyyār - (valiyavar nilaitappi viḻuntāl) vēṟu enta vakaiyilum piḻaikka māṭṭār; noyya nilaitappi caḻakkeṉṟu vīḻāvām - pāramillāta poruḷ nilai tavaṟi viraivil viḻamāṭṭā; vīḻiṉum uyyum - viḻuntālum piḻaikkum; piṟa uyyā -(pāramāṉa) piṟaporuḷkaḷ piḻaikkamāṭṭā.
ciṟumaiyum perumaiyum celvam atikāram pōṉṟavaikaḷaik kuṟikkum.
(ka.u)ciṟiyōr nilai tappik kīḻnilai aṭaiyiṉum piḻaippar: mēlōr nilai tavaṟik kīḻ nilai aṭaintāl uyyamāṭṭār.
periyar ciṟiyar ceykaikaḷ
icaiyāta pōliṉum mēlaiyōr ceykai 
vacaiyākā maṟṟaiyōrk kallāl - pacu vēṭṭut
tīyōmpi vāṉvaḻakkaṅ kāṇpārai yoppavē
ūṉōmpi ūṉṟiṉ pavar. 97
97. The conduct of the exalted is not to be reprehended like that of others, although it may not appear praiseworthy. For, are those, who after sacrificing a cow, and cherishing the sacred fire, behold the ways of heaven, to be likened to those who eat flesh to pamper their bodies?
(pa-u) mēlaiyōr ceykai icaiyāta pōliṉum - periyōriṉ ceykaikaḷ poruttamillātavai pōlak kāṇappaṭṭālum; maṟṟaiyōrkku allāl vacaiyākā -(avar pōlallāta) ciṟiyōrkku allātu vacaiyākamāṭṭātu; pacu vēṭṭu tī ōmpi vāṉ vaḻakkam kāṇpārai oppavē - pacuvaip pali koṭuttu tīyai vaḷarttu vāṉulaka iyalpukaḷ kāṇpavarai oppavarāvar; ūṉ ōmpi ūṉ tiṉpavar - uṭampaip pātukākkum poruṭṭu iṟaicciyait tiṉpavar.
vāṉ vaḻakkaṅ kāṇpār eṉpataṟku - maḻai peyyac ceyvār eṉṟum poruḷ kūṟalām. ivvāciriyar uyirkaḷaik koṉṟu vēḷvi ceytal kuṟṟattiṉpāṟpaṭāteṉa nampiyiruntār. ik karuttu ellārukkum uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu.
(ka-u) periyār ceyalkaḷum ciṟiyār ceyalkaḷum cila camayaṅkaḷil ottaṉapōṟ kāṇappaṭiṉum avaikaḷiṉ nōkkaṅkaḷ vevvēṟaṉa.
periyār taṉmai 
evareva rettiṟattar attiṟatta rāyniṉ
ṟavaravark kāvaṉa kūṟi - evarevarkkum
uppālāy niṟpamaṟ ṟemmuṭaiyār tammuṭaiyāṉ
eppālum niṟpa teṉa. 98
98. Our spiritual preceptors, who adapt themselves to the disposition of each, whatever it be, and give to everyone suitable counsel, will stand beyond all others, as their Lord is everywhere present.
(pa-u) em uṭaiyār - emmai āḷukiṉṟa periyavar; tam uṭaiyāṉ eppālum niṟpatu eṉa - tammai āḷukiṉṟa kaṭavuḷ ellār taṉmaikaḷukkumēṟpa iruppatupōla; evar evar ettiṟattar attiṟattar āy niṉṟu avar avarkku āvaṉa kūṟi-yār yār ettaṉmaiyarāyirunta pōtilum avvattaṉmaikkēṟpa niṉṟu ākavēṇṭiyavaṟṟaik kūṟi; uppālāy niṟpa - ellārkkum mēlāka niṟpar.
(ka-u) periyōr maṟṟavarkaḷ taṉmaikkēṟpa avaravarkkāvaṉa kūṟittām mēlāka niṟpar.
meyyuṇarntār ceyal
meyyuṇarntār poymmēṟpulam pōkkār meyyuṇarcci
kaivarutal kaṇṇāp pulaṅkāppār - meyyuṇarntār
kāppē nilaiyāp paḻi nāṇal nīḷkatavāc 
cērppar niṟaittāḻ ceṟittu. 99 
99. They who know the truth, indulge not their senses in the pursuit of lies, and have in view the acquisition of true knowledge; will join to the door post of caution, the long door of the shame of sin, and will secure it with the bolt of steadfastness.
(pa-u) mey uṇarntār - uṇmaipporuḷkaḷai uṇarntavar; poy mēl pulam pōkkār - poyyāṉa poruḷkaḷ mītu pulaṉkaḷaic celuttār; mey uṇarcci kaivarutal kaṇṇāpulam kāppār - mey aṟivu uṇṭātal karuttākap pulaṉkaḷai aṭakkuvār; meyyuṇarntār kāppē nilaiā - meyyuṇarn tavarkaḷatu (pulaṉkaḷaik kākkum) kāppē nilaiyākavum; paḻi nāṇal ē nīḷ katavā - paḻikku nāṇum kuṇattai nīṇṭa katavākavum; niṟai tāḻ ceṟittucērppar - niṟainta kuṇamākiya tāḻppāḷai iṟukappōṭuvar.
(ka-u) uṇmaip poruḷkaḷai aṟintavar pulaṉkaḷai oṭukkuvar.
tuṟavikaḷiṉ iṉpam
kaṟṟut tuṟaipōya kātalaṟkuk kaṟpiṉāḷ
peṟṟuk koṭutta talaimakaṉ pōl - muṟṟat
tuṟantārkku meyyuṇarviṟ ṟōṉṟuvatē iṉpam
iṟantavellām tuṉpamalā til. 100 
100. As the first born son presented by a virtuous wife, to a husband, who has attained the shore of the sea of knowledge, is the bliss which appears in the contemplation of truth to those who have entirely renounced the world: compared to which all, that is past is but pain.
(pa-u) kaṟṟu tuṟaipōya kātalaṟku - kaṟkavēṇṭiyavaṟṟaik kaṟṟu muṭitta kaṇavaṉukku; kaṟpiṉāḷ peṟṟu koṭutta talaimakaṉ pōl - kaṟpuḷḷa maṉaivi peṟṟuk koṭutta mutalmakaṉaip pōl; muṟṟa tuṟantārkku meyyuṇarvil tōṉṟuvatē iṉpam - muṟṟākat tuṟavaṭaintavarkku uṇmai aṟiviṉāl tōṉṟuvatē iṉpamākum; iṟanta ellām tuṉpam alātu il - aḵtu allāta maṟṟavai ellām tuṉpam allāmal vēṟu illai.
(ka-u) tuṟavikaḷukku meyyuṇarvē iṉpam aḷikkum.
vīṭṭuvakai aṭaippavar
kaṟṟāṅ kaṟintaṭaṅkit tītorīi naṉṟāṟṟip
peṟṟatu koṇṭu maṉantiruntip - paṟṟuvatē
paṟṟuvatē paṟṟip paṇiyaṟaniṉ ṟoṉṟuṇarntu
niṟpārē nīṇeṟic ceṉṟār. 101 
101. They, who have studied and attained knowledge; have exercised self-control; have rejected evil and embraced good; have contented their desires with what they have obtained; and thus by laying hold on their object, have superseded the necessity of works; and employ their thoughts in the contemplation of the one object only, walk in the endless path of happiness.
(pa-u) kaṟṟu āṅku aṟintu - nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟu avaikaḷiṉ uṇmaip poruḷai aṟintu; aṭaṅki - aṭakkamāyiruntu; tītu ōrīi - tīmaiyai nīkki; naṉṟu āṟṟi - nalla kāriyaṅkaḷaic ceytu; peṟṟatu koṇṭu maṉam tirutti - kiṭaittavaṟṟaik koṇṭu maṉam niṟai vaṭaintu; paṟṟuvatē paṟṟuvatē paṟṟi - tām aṭaiyavēṇṭiya neṟi kaḷaik kaikkoṇṭu; paṇi aṟam niṉṟu - vitikkappaṭṭa aṟa vaḻikaḷil niṉṟu; oṉṟu uṇarntu - kaṭavuḷai aṟintu; niṟpārē nīḷ neṟic ceṉṟār - niṟkiṉṟavarē vīṭṭu neṟiyil niṟkiṉṟavarāvar.
(ka-u) nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟu ataṉpaṭi oḻukik kaṭavuḷai aṟintavar vīṭṭu ulakai aṭaipavarāvar.
aiyan tiripiṉ ṟaḷantut tiyiṟṟeḷintu
meyyuṇarccik kaṇviḻippat tūṅkuvōr - tammuḷē
kāṇpatē kāṭci kaṉavu naṉavākap
pūṇpatē tīrnta poruḷ. 102 
102. The vision of those, who having measured without doubt and error, and ascertained all things by reason, sleep to external things that the eye of true knowledge may be awake, which looks within themselves, and substitutes spiritual, for material subjects, is the final and perfect attainment.
(pa-u) aiyam tiripu iṉṟu aḷantu - cantēkamum māṟupāṭum illāmal ñāyattiṉāl aḷantu; uttiyil teḷintu - maṉatil teḷintu; mey uṇarcci kaṇ viḻippa tūṅkuvōr - meyyaṟivākiya kaṇ viḻit tirukka veḷikkarumaṅkaḷil tūkkam koṇṭiruppavar; tam uḷḷē kāṇ patē kāṭci - tamatu maṉatil kāṇpatē kāṭciyākum; kaṉavu naṉavāka pūṇpatē tīrnta poruḷ - (ñāṉamākiya nittiraiyil kāṇum) kaṉavai viḻippu nilaiyilum kaṇṭu niṟṟalē muṭinta muṭivāṉa poruḷākum.
